\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{i}]
we mean the sum
of
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{i}]_{k}
up to order 11.

Similarly, we use
\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{i}]_{k}

and
\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{i}]_{k}
to denote the norm of the
k
-th
derivative of
\Psi_{i}
along null hypersurfaces
\mathcal{N}_{u}

and
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}
respectively, and likewise
use
\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{i}]
to denote the sum of
\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{i}]_{k}
up
to 10th order. By
\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{i}]
we mean the sum
of
\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{i}]_{k}
up to 10th order.

We also make use of the
initial data
quantity

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\equiv\sup_{0\leq v\leq 1}\mathcal{I}_{0}(v),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}(v)\equiv\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{15}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\mathchar 1278\relax^{j}(|u_{\infty}|\mathcal{D})^{i}(\sigma,\varphi_{0})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}.

Notation.
In the rest of the article, for simplicity of the
presentation, we use
A\lesssim B
to denote that there exist a
constant
C>0
which is independent of
a
, such that
A\leq CB
. We
do not distinguish between
\lesssim
and
\leq
when there is no
source of ambiguity. In addition, we write
\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}

to denote
\mathchar 1264\relax^{j_{1}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{j_{2}}\Gamma...\mathchar 1264\relax^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma

where
i_{1}\geq 0
,
i_{2}\geq 1
,
j_{1}
,
j_{2}
, …,

j_{i_{2}}\in\mathbb{N}
and
j_{1}+j_{2}+...+j_{i_{2}}=i_{1}
.

4.4
Strategy of the bootstrap argument

The main theorem in this article relies on an
a priori
estimate
for the nonlinear Einstein-scalar system. In order to derive uniform
upper bounds on
{\bm{\Gamma}}
,
\bm{\Psi}
and
{\bm{\varphi}}
, we make use of
a bootstrap argument. From the analysis of the initial data for the
CIVP,
{\bm{\Gamma}}_{0}
,
\bm{\Psi}_{0}
and
{\bm{\varphi}}_{0}
we readily have the
bound

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}_{0}+\bm{\Psi}_{0}+{\bm{\varphi}}_{0}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}.

(34)

The proof of our main theorem will show that in the area

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}=\big\{(u,v)|u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4,\ \ 0\leq v\leq 1\big\}

the inequality

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}(u,v)+\bm{\Psi}(u,v)+{\bm{\varphi}}(u,v)\lesssim(\mathcal{I}_{0})^{2}+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1,

(35)

holds. In turn, the above uniform bound leads to a last slice argument
from which it follows that a solution to Einstein-scalar system exists
within the area
\mathbb{D}
.

In order to establish (
35
), we make the bootstrap
assumptions

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+{\bm{\varphi}}\leq\mathcal{O}.

(36)

for large
\mathcal{O}
such that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\ll\mathcal{O}

but also

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{20}\leq a^{\frac{1}{16}}.

Letting

\displaystyle\bm{I}=\{u\,|\,u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4,\ \eqref{Hypothesis}\ \textrm{holds for every}\ 0\leq v\leq 1\},

our goal becomes to show that, in fact,

\bm{I}=[u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4]
. We will follow the

standard bootstrap principle
(or
continuity method
) to
derive this conclusion —i.e, showing that
\bm{I}
is both open and
closed.

Our argument is naturally divided into the following steps:

Step 1
: From the assumptions on the initial data, Condition
(
34
), and the local existence for the Einstein-scalar
system, the existence area can always be slightly extended
in
u
. Then there exists a small
\varepsilon
such that for

u_{\infty}\leq u\leq u_{\infty}+\varepsilon
we have

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+{\bm{\varphi}}\lesssim 2\mathcal{I}_{0}\ll\mathcal{O}.

This implies
that
[u_{\infty}\leq u\leq u_{\infty}+\varepsilon]\subseteq\bm{I}
,
and it follows that
\bm{I}
is a closed set.

Step 2
: In Sections 5, 6 and 7 we show the estimates

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}(u,v)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}(u,v)^{2}+\bm{\Psi}(u,v)+\bm{\varphi}(u,v)^{2}+\bm{\varphi}(u,v)+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1,

\displaystyle\bm{\varphi}(u,v)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}+1,\quad\bm{\Psi}(u,v)\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}+1.

The above estimates improve the upper bounds in the bootstrap
assumption (
36
). Accordingly,
\bm{I}
can be extended a
bit forward along
u
. This implies that
\bm{I}
is open.

Step 3
: Using local existence and basic topology, it follows
from Steps 1 and 2 that the only possibility for the set
\bm{I}
to
be both open and closed in
[u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4]
is that it is
the whole set. We conclude
that
\bm{I}\equiv[u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4]
and, moreover, that the
uniform bound in (
35
) holds.

4.5
Estimates for the components of the frame

In order to kick-start the construction of our main estimates we need
first some basic estimates for the components of the frame in terms of
the basic bootstrap assumptions (
36
).

Step 1.
Integrating the definition of
{\underline{\chi}}
in
the
v
-direction and making use of the bootstrap
assumption (
36
) for
\underline{\chi}
, one has that

\displaystyle|Q-1|=\left|\int_{0}^{1}{\underline{\chi}}\mathrm{d}v\right|\leq\int_{0}^{1}||{\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{1}{|u|}||\underline{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}.

In the above estimate it has been used that
Q=1

on
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}\equiv\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{\star}
where Minkowski data
is given. From the assumption that
a
is suitably larger
than
\mathcal{O}
, one has
that
||Q,Q^{-1}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}
are close to
1
.

Step 2.
Making use of the estimate
(
22
)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}v}\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}\phi=\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}Q^{-1}\left(D\phi-(\rho+\bar{\rho})\phi\right)

and letting
\phi=1
one concludes that

\displaystyle\mbox{Area}(S_{u,v})-\mbox{Area}(S_{u,0})\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}2Q^{-1}||\rho||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}.

Now, making use of the bootstrap assumption
of
||\rho||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\mathcal{O}/|u|
and
that
\mbox{Area}(S_{u,0})=2\pi u^{2}
we obtain

\displaystyle\mbox{Area}(S_{u,v})-2\pi u^{2}\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\mbox{Area}(S_{u,v^{\prime}}).

Finally, making use of the Grönwall inequality we find that

\displaystyle|\mbox{Area}(S_{u,v})-2\pi u^{2}|\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}.

4.6
Grönwall-type estimates

One of the basic tools in our analysis will be a Grönwall-type
estimate on transport equations. We begin by noticing the following:

Proposition 1
.

Under the bootstrap assumption (
36
), we have that

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1278\relax\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime},

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{0},v})}+\int_{u_{0}}^{u}||\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

Proof.

Let
f\equiv|\phi|^{2}
. Making use of the discussion in
Section
3.3
and the bootstrap assumption
on
\rho
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}v}||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq||\mathchar 1278\relax\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

Then, the Grönwall inequality will give us that

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\right)\left(||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{1}||\mathchar 1278\relax\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{1}||\mathchar 1278\relax\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}.

Along the
u
direction, making use of the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle||\tilde{\mu}||_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}},

where
\tilde{\mu}\equiv\mu+\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}
, we observe
that

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{|u|}\leq\mu\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{|u|}\leq 0.

Hence, when
f\equiv|\phi|^{2}
, using that
Q\sim 1
we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq||\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

from which the desired result follows.
∎

We also have the following scale-invariant norm version of the last result:

Proposition 2
.

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1278\relax\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime},

(37a)

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{0,v})}+\int_{0}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}.

(37b)

In the following we focus attention on the transport equations for the
components of the connection in the incoming direction. Schematically,
these can be written as

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Gamma=\lambda_{0}\mu\Gamma+F.

In view of the important role to be played by the bootstrap assumption
on
\mu
, we will require more precise estimates. In particular, we
have the following result:

Proposition 3
.

For transport equations of the form

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Gamma=\lambda_{0}\mu\Gamma+F,

one has the estimate

\displaystyle|u|^{\lambda_{1}}||\Gamma||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim|u_{\infty}|^{\lambda_{1}}||\Gamma||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{\prime}|^{\lambda_{1}}||F||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime},

where

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}\equiv-(\lambda_{0}+1).

Proof.

Let
f\equiv|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2}
and apply equation

23
. The left-hand side of this equation is then given by

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}f=\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}=2|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

The integrands on the right-hand side satisfy

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}(|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2})+2\mu|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\lambda_{1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}-1}\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}(|u|)|\Gamma|^{2}+|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}(\bar{\Gamma}\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Gamma+\Gamma\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\bar{\Gamma})+2\mu|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-2\lambda_{1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}-1}|\Gamma|^{2}+2\lambda_{0}\mu|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2}+2|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma||F|+2\mu|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2}

\displaystyle=-2\lambda_{1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|^{2}(\mu-\frac{1}{u})+2|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\Gamma F.

Now, making use of the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle||\mu-\frac{1}{u}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}},

one can show that the right-hand side is less than

\displaystyle 2\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+2|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|F|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

Putting everything together one gets

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|F|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

Integrating with respect to
u
and applying the Grönwall
inequality, one concludes that

\displaystyle|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\exp{\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}}\right)\left(|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|||u^{\prime}|^{\lambda_{1}}|F|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim|||u|^{\lambda_{1}}|\Gamma|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|||u^{\prime}|^{\lambda_{1}}|F|||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

∎

In terms of scale-invariant norms the above result can be reformulated
as

Proposition 4
.

For transport equations of the form

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Gamma=\lambda_{0}\mu\Gamma+F,

one has that

\displaystyle a^{s_{2}(\Gamma)}|u|^{\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\Gamma)}||\Gamma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim a^{s_{2}(\Gamma)}|u_{\infty}|^{\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\Gamma)}||\Gamma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a^{s_{2}(F)}|u^{\prime}|^{\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)}||F||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime},

where
s_{2}(F)=s_{2}(\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Gamma)=s_{2}(\Gamma)+1
.

4.7
Sobolev embedding

Exploiting the fact that
\mbox{Area}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})\sim|u|^{2}
and
working under the bootstrap assumptions, the Sobolev embedding
inequalities (
25
) and (
26
) imply the
following:

Proposition 5
.

Under the bootstrap assumptions (
36
) one has

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 2}|||u|^{i-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

(38)

In terms of scale-invariant norms one has that

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L_{sc}^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 2}||\left(a^{1/2}\mathcal{D}\right)^{i}\phi||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

(39)

4.8
Commutators

Suppose that the T-weighted quantity
f
satisfies the transport
equation
\mathchar 1278\relax f=H_{0}
. Then, using Stewart’s gauge and the
commutator (
65b
) we have that

\displaystyle H_{k}=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}H_{0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}f,

where
H_{k}\equiv\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}f
. Similarly, suppose
f

satisfies
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}f=G_{0}
, we have that

\displaystyle G_{k}=-k\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}f+\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}G_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}f+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\lambda\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}f,

where
G_{k}\equiv\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}f
. In the above expressions for
H_{k}

and
G_{k}
we need give neither the exact constants in each sum nor the
T-weight of
f
. Furthermore, in the expressions for
H_{k}
and
G_{k}

we need not distinguish the
\mathchar 1264\relax
and
\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}
operators or their
complex conjugates. The justification for this can be traced back to
the definition of the norms.

5
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates

In this section, under the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}},\bm{\Psi},\bm{\varphi}\leq\mathcal{O},

we show
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates of the connection coefficients,
the auxiliary field
\varphi_{a}
and the Weyl curvature. In particular
we demonstrate the inequality

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}^{2}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}^{2}+\bm{\varphi}+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1.

Note that
{\bm{\Gamma}}
contains up to 10 derivatives of the connection
coefficients, 10 derivatives of
\varphi_{0,1,2}
and 9 derivatives of
the Weyl scalars
\Psi_{0,...,4}
. We use the 10th derivative of the
Weyl terms with the 11th derivative of
\varphi_{0,1,2}
on the
lightcone to control
{\bm{\Gamma}}
.

The general strategy of the proof is to make use of the transport
equations satisfied by the various quantities involved in the field
equations. Then one applies the commutator relations into either
equation (
37a
) or the inequalities in
Proposition
4
. Making use of scale-invariant
norms, we show in detail that the main contributions in the transport
equations come from the linear terms, and a handful of nonlinear terms
involving the scalar field. The T-weight of the various fields allows
us to obtain estimates for
\mathcal{D}^{k}f
once we have an estimate
on one of its strings of derivatives. For concreteness, in our
calculations we always choose
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}f
as an example. In the course
of calculation, we make frequent use of the bootstrap
assumption (
36
) and the fact
that
\mathcal{O}\ll a\leq|u|
. In the case of the connection
coefficients we can always find structure equations which do not
involve angular derivatives. In contrast, for
\varphi_{0,1,2}
and
the components of the Weyl tensor we instead transform the estimates
of angular derivatives from
\mathcal{S}
to lightcone estimates.

5.1
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates of connection coefficients

We start by looking at the estimates for the connection
coefficients. Our first result is the following:

Proposition 6
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

We illustrate the method of proof by looking in detail at the case
of the spin connection coefficient
\lambda
. In this case the
relevant structure equation
is (
28h
) —namely

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\lambda=-2\mu\lambda-{\underline{\omega}}\lambda-\Psi_{4}.

In order to estimate
||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||
one
needs to commute each
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
. We
take
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\lambda
as an example. One has that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\lambda+(k+2)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\lambda=-\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{4}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\tilde{\mu}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\lambda+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\lambda.

Denote the right-hand side of the last equation by
F
. One has that

\displaystyle s_{2}(\lambda)=1,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\lambda)=\frac{k+2}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+4}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+2),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k+1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\lambda)=-1.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

Multiplying by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
one has

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

Now, for convenience let

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

Substituting the expression of
F
one finds that

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tilde{\mu}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\lambda)(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-1}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

For
I_{1}
it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Psi_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

Now, making use of the bootstrap assumptions

\displaystyle||\tilde{\mu}||\leq\frac{|u|}{a}\mathcal{O},\quad||\lambda||\leq\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O},\quad||{\underline{\omega}},\tau||\leq\mathcal{O},\quad||\mu||\leq\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O},

for
I_{2}
and
I_{3}
, the above assumptions give

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}}{|u|}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

Combining the results above with those on the initial data, one
concludes

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

One can estimate the rest of the strings in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\lambda

in analogous way. Hence, from Definition (
18
), we
have

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

∎

Proposition 7
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

Proof.

In this case we make use of the transport
equation (
28b
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\sigma

\displaystyle=2\rho\sigma+\Psi_{0}

to estimate
\sigma
. For
k\leq 10
, commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}

with
\mathchar 1278\relax
, one has that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\sigma

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Psi_{0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\sigma.

Then, making use of the transport estimate in scale-invariant norm,
equation (
37a
), one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}.

Note that the initial data for
\sigma
on the initial ingoing light
cone is Minkowskian —i.e, it is zero. Now, multiplying
by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
both sides of the inequality we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+1}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}+1}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

The rest of the strings in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\sigma
can be estimated
in a similar way. Hence, from Definition (
18
), we
conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

∎

Proposition 8
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

Here we make use of the structure
equation (
28k
) —namely,

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle=2\tau\bar{\tau}+2\tau\pi+2\bar{\tau}\bar{\pi}+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}.

For
k\leq 10
, we commute
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
, to obtain

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\varphi_{2}.

Then, the transport estimate in the scale-invariant norm,
inequality (
37a
), yields

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}.

As the initial data for
{\underline{\omega}}
on the initial ingoing light cone
is Minkowskian (and thus vanishes), we have

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+1}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+1}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})+1

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

where in the last step we have made use of the
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})

estimates for
\varphi_{0}
and
\varphi_{2}
which will be shown in
Proposition
14
and Proposition
15
.

The remaining strings of derivatives in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}{\underline{\omega}}

can be estimated in a similar way. Hence, from
Definition
18
, we conclude that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

Proposition 9
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of the transport equation (
33
) —namely

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}{\underline{\chi}}=2\tau\pi+2\bar{\tau}\bar{\pi}+2\pi\bar{\pi}+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+{\underline{\omega}}{\underline{\chi}}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
with
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\chi}}+k\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\chi}}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\pi+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}{\underline{\omega}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}{\underline{\chi}}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{2}.

Denote the right-hand side of the above equation by
F
. One has that

\displaystyle s_{2}({\underline{\chi}})=0,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\chi}})=\frac{k}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+2}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-k,\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k-1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\underline{\chi}})=-1.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,{\underline{\omega}})\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Gamma(\pi,{\underline{\chi}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

The term
I_{1}
can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\underline{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\frac{\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]}{|u|}.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{i}\varphi_{0}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k-i}\varphi_{2}||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(\varphi_{0})\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma(\varphi_{2})\leq\frac{\Gamma(\varphi_{0})\Gamma(\varphi_{2})}{|u|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1}{|u|}.

The last step in the above chain of inequalities will be shown in the
Propositions
14
and
15
.

Finally, for
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{i}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k-i}\Gamma||\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}.

Collecting the previous estimates, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]}{|u|}+\frac{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1}{|u|},

from which it follows that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\Psi}[\bm{{\tilde{\Psi}}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

Proposition 10
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of the structure equation (
28i
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\tau

\displaystyle=(\tau+\bar{\pi})\rho+(\bar{\tau}+\pi)\sigma+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}.

For
k\leq 10
, commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\tau

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\varphi_{1}.

Next, we make use of the transport estimate in scale-invariant norm
(
37a
). One concludes that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}.

As the initial data of
\tau
on the initial ingoing light cone is
Minkowskian, we have that
\tau=0
. Accordingly, we find that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+1}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+1}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}\leq\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+1.

The above leads to

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+1

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

Proposition 11
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1)^{2}.

Proof.

We make use of the structure equation for
\rho
—namely,
(
28a
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\rho

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}+\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2}.

For
k\leq 10
, commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\rho

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\bar{\sigma}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\rho\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\rho

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\sigma+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\rho.

Next, we make use of the transport estimate in terms of
scale-invariant norms, inequality (
37a
). One
finds that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma),\varphi_{0})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}(\Gamma(\rho,\sigma),\varphi_{0})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\lesssim 1+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{2}+\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{2}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq 1+\frac{a}{|u|}\left({\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{2}+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{2}\right)+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}+\frac{a\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1)^{2}.

Observe that in the third inequality one has
that
i_{2}\neq 0
. Similarly, one can estimate the rest of the strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\rho
and obtain the same result. We conclude
then that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1)^{2}.

∎

Remark 14
.

The initial data of
\rho
on the ingoing light cone is
Minkowskian —that is, one has
\rho=-\frac{1}{|u|}
. In terms of scale-invariant
norms we have

\displaystyle||\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}=1;\quad\sum_{1\leq k\leq 10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}=0.

Hence, when
1\leq k\leq 9
, we have that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\left({\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{2}+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{2}\right)+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}+\frac{a\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}.

Proposition 12
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

Proof.

In this case we make use of the ingoing equation of
{\tilde{\mu}}
—namely,

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}{\tilde{\mu}}+2\mu{\tilde{\mu}}={\tilde{\mu}}^{2}-{\underline{\omega}}\mu-\lambda\bar{\lambda}-3\varphi_{2}^{2}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}+(k+2)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}{\underline{\omega}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\mu+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{2}.

Denoting the right-hand side of the above equation by
F
it follows
then that

\displaystyle s_{2}({\tilde{\mu}})=1,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}})=\frac{k+2}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+4}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+2),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k+1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\lambda)=-1.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mu\mathcal{D}^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}{\tilde{\mu}}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}{\bm{\Gamma}}({\underline{\omega}})+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\left(\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{a}{|u|}({\bm{\Gamma}}({\underline{\omega}})+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})^{2})

\displaystyle\leq 1+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}],

so that, in the end, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

so that, consequently,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

∎

Proposition 13
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of the structure
equation (
28j
), that is

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\pi=-(\pi+\bar{\tau})\mu-(\bar{\pi}+\tau)\lambda-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\pi+(k+1)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\pi

\displaystyle=-\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\bar{\tau}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{2}.

Denoting the right-hand side of the above expression by
F
, it follows
that

\displaystyle s_{2}(\pi)=\frac{1}{2},\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\pi)=\frac{k+1}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+3}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+1),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\pi)=-1.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
one has

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

Multiplying by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mu\mathcal{D}^{k}\bar{\tau}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}(\tau,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+I_{1}+...+I_{4}.

Now, for the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}].

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\mu||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\bar{\tau}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\Gamma(\tau)\lesssim\frac{\bm{\Gamma}(\tau)}{|u|}.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}.

Finally, for
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})\leq\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})}{|u|}.

Now, combining
I_{1}
and
I_{4}
we see that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Gamma}(\tau)+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})+1}{|u|},

which leads to

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Gamma}(\tau)+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})+1

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

5.2
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates of the auxiliary field
\varphi_{a}

Next, we proceed to discuss the construction of estimates for the
components of the auxiliary field.

Proposition 14
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We make use of equation (
27a
),

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{0}-\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}

\displaystyle=({\underline{\omega}}-\mu)\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}-\bar{\tau}\varphi_{1}-\tau\bar{\varphi}_{1}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}+(k+1)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle=-\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{0}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\rho,\tau)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}(\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2}).

Denoting the right-hand side of the previous equation by
F
, one has that

\displaystyle s_{2}(\varphi_{0})=0,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\pi)=\frac{k}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+2}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+1),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-2,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0})=0.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one has

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})},

so that multiplying by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
one sees

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma(\rho,\tau)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}(\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle=1+I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

For the term
I_{2}
we have

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}})a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}(\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}})\leq 1.

And, finally, for the term
I_{3}
it can be verified that

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1)\mathcal{O}^{2}=\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}(\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}+\frac{a\mathcal{O}^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}})\leq 1.

Combining the above estimates we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}+1\leq\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

A similar analysis of all the other strings of derivatives
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\varphi_{0}
leads to the same result, so that we
can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

The components
\varphi_{1}
and
\varphi_{2}
can be analysed
together. We have the following:

Proposition 15
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

Proof.

In this proof we make use of the definition

\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{2}\equiv\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{2}+\mu\bar{\varphi}_{1},

and the associated bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\mathcal{O},

for
0\leq k\leq 9
.

Starting from equation (
27c
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}+2\mu\varphi_{1}=\bar{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}-\bar{\lambda}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

we commute
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}+(k+2)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\bar{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{1},

where
0\leq k\leq 10
. Denote by
F
the right-hand side of the
previous equation. One has that

\displaystyle s_{2}(\varphi_{1})=\frac{1}{2},\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1})=\frac{k+1}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+3}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+2),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k+1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-2,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1})=0.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one has that

\displaystyle a^{\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})},

so that
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
to both hands side and substituting the
expression for
F
leads to

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\varphi_{1}||

\displaystyle\leq 1+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}].

Finally, observing that the rest of the strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\varphi_{1}
give rise to the same estimate, one
can conclude that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+1.

For the analysis of
{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}
, we apply
\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}

to (
27d
) and make use of the commutator
relations to yield

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\tilde{\varphi}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax\pi+\bar{\pi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+2\pi\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}-\bar{\tau}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}-\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mu+\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\pi+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\pi}+\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\rho

\displaystyle+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\tilde{\varphi}_{2}+2\rho\tilde{\varphi}_{2}+\bar{\sigma}\bar{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}+\pi^{2}\varphi_{1}+2\pi\bar{\pi}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+\pi\rho\varphi_{2}+\lambda\sigma\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\pi\bar{\tau}\varphi_{1}-\bar{\pi}\bar{\tau}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\rho\bar{\tau}\varphi_{2}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
we find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime k+2}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{4}+1}\Gamma(\pi,\mu,\rho)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{3}}(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma,\varphi_{1})\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{4}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{4}}\Gamma(\sigma,\tau,\pi,\rho)\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime i_{5}}\bm{\varphi},

where
0\leq k\leq 9
. Making use of the transport estimate in
scale-invariant norm (
37a
) it follows that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+2}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma(\pi,\mu,\rho)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma,\varphi_{1})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\sigma,\tau,\pi,\rho)\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=1+I_{1}+...+I_{5}.

Now, for
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+2}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}=\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{k+1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+2}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]\leq 1.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\big(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a}\mathcal{O}+\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\big)\leq 1.

For
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\leq 1.

Finally, for
I_{5}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\big(\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}+\mathcal{O}^{2}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\big)\leq 1.

Combining the above estimates for
I_{1},\ldots I_{5}
we see that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Following the same strategy one can estimate the rest of the strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}
to conclude that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

From the definition of
{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}
we have
\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{2}=\tilde{\varphi}_{2}-\mu\bar{\varphi}_{1}
, then

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}(\mu\varphi_{1})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq 1+\frac{a}{|u|}\frac{1}{|u|}||\mu||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\frac{a}{|u|}\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mu||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\varphi_{1}||

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq 1+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\frac{|u|}{a}\mathcal{O}\leq 1.

when
0\leq k\leq 9
.

In the case of
k=0
we fall back into
equation (
27d
) and make use of the
transport estimate in scale-invariant norm

\displaystyle||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1278\relax\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})},

so that multiplying by
a^{\frac{1}{2}}/|u|
we can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1278\relax\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}(||\mathcal{D}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+||\mu\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}+\pi\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}+\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq 1+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]\leq 1.

In the last step of the previous chain of inequalities we have made
use of

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathcal{D}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a}{|u|}\mathcal{O}\leq 1.

Collecting the above estimates for
\varphi_{2}
we finally conclude
that

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1

for
0\leq k\leq 10
.
∎

5.3
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates of Weyl curvature

Next in our hierarchy, we construct sphere estimates for the
components of the Weyl tensor.

Proposition 16
.

For
0\leq k\leq 9
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

We make use of the Bianchi identity (
29b
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{0}+\mu\Psi_{0}-\mathchar 1264\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}=6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{1}+2{\underline{\omega}}\Psi_{0}-5\tau{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+3\sigma{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}\sigma-3\varphi_{0}^{2}\bar{\lambda}-3\varphi_{1}^{2}\rho-6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\sigma-12\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\tau.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
, we find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}+(k+1)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Psi_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}.

Denoting the right-hand side of the previous equality by
F
, one has

\displaystyle s_{2}(\Psi_{0})=0,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0})=\frac{k}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+2}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+1),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-2,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0})=0.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one finds

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})},

so that multiplying by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
one concludes that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\sigma)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+I_{1}+...+I_{5}.

For
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}].

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}(\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}+\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}})a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}\leq 1.

For
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\mathcal{O}^{2}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1,

and, finally, for
I_{5}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

Combining the above estimates for
I_{1},\dots,I_{5}
we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1\leq 1.

Observing that for the remaining strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\varphi_{2}
one obtains the same result, we can
conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

∎

Proposition 17
.

For
0\leq k\leq 9
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

For the
L^{2}
estimates of
{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}
,
{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}
,
{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}

and
\Psi_{4}
, we make use of the Bianchi
identities (
29a
),
(
29c
),
(
29e
) and
(
29g
). These equations share the same
structure —namely, one has that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\Psi_{II}=\mathchar 1264\relax(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})+\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax\bm{\varphi}+\Gamma\Psi+\Gamma\bm{\varphi}\bm{\varphi},

where we have used
{\tilde{\Psi}}
denote
\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}\}

and
\Psi_{II}
to denote
\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}
. We
commute
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}+1}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Psi+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}.

We then make use of the scale-invariant norm transport
estimate (
37a
) so as to obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k,i_{3}<k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}+1}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+I_{1}+...+I_{5}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}]\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

For
I_{2}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}(||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0,1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{0,1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}+\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For the term
I_{4}
, the worst term is
\lambda\Psi_{0}
. In this case we
have

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\leq 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

Finally, for
I_{5}
, we denote integral contain
terms
\mu\varphi_{0,1}\varphi_{0,1}
by
I_{51}
. One has that

\displaystyle I_{51}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mu\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{0,1}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{0,1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}<k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\mu\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi_{0,1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\varphi_{0,1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}
