\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(\varphi_{0,1})a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(\varphi_{0,1})+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|^{2}}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}

\displaystyle\leq{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+\frac{1}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Similarly, denote the integral contain terms with
\varphi_{2}
,
namely
\sigma\varphi_{2}^{2}
,
\lambda\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}
and

\pi\varphi_{1}\varphi_{2}
by
I_{52}
. It follows then that

\displaystyle I_{52}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\left(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}+\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}+\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{3}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

Denoting the remaining terms by
I_{53}
, one has

\displaystyle I_{53}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,\sigma,\tau,\pi,\rho)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi_{0,1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\varphi_{0,1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\left(\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}+\mathcal{O}\right)a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}+3}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

To conclude, combining the above estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{5}
one
has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

An inspection then shows that the other strings of derivatives
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\Psi_{II}
give rise to the same
estimate. Accordingly, we find that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

5.4
Summary and discussion of
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimate

In this subsection we summarise and put together
the
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates we have obtained so far.

We have showed that under the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}},\bm{\varphi},\bm{\Psi}\leq\mathcal{O},

we obtain the estimates

(i)

for
0\leq k\leq 10
,

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1)^{2},

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

(ii)

for
0\leq k\leq 10
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,\quad||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

(iii)

for
0\leq k\leq 9
,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

where
\Psi_{II}=\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}
.

Actually, from the bootstrap assumption
{\bm{\Gamma}}\leq\mathcal{O}
, one
can obtain additional estimates for all derivatives of
\bm{\varphi}

and
\bm{\Psi}
except those at the highest level. We now give estimates
which will be used to control the Gaussian curvature.

When
0\leq k\leq 10
, from Propositions
14

and
15
we have that

\displaystyle\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]_{k}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{a}\left(\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1,

and furthermore that

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]_{k}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{a}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

When
0\leq k\leq 9
, from Proposition
16
we have that

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]_{k}=\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}=1.

With the aid of the above three results, one can make use of
the
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates from the previous subsection, so that
when
0\leq k\leq 9
one has that

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}(\sigma,\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1})_{k}\lesssim 1.

Moreover, making use of the
estimate (
29c
) in
Proposition
17
to estimate
\Psi_{2}
, we can conclude with
the inequality

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}({\tilde{\Psi}}_{2})_{k}\leq 1,

which leads to

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]_{k}\leq 1,\quad\bm{\Gamma}({\tilde{\mu}})_{k}\leq 1.

From Lemma
14
we have that for
1\leq k\leq 9

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}(\rho)_{k}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}

and

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}(\rho)_{0}\lesssim 1.

Collecting the results above we have following corollary:

Corollary 1
.

Under bootstrap assumption

{\bm{\Gamma}},\bm{\varphi},\bm{\Psi}\leq\mathcal{O}
, for
0\leq k\leq 9
, we
have that

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}(\sigma,{\tilde{\mu}},\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2})_{k},\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]_{k},\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]_{k}\lesssim 1.

When
0\leq k\leq 10
, we have that

\displaystyle\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]_{k},\,\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]_{k}\leq 1,

and for
\rho
we have that

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}(\rho)_{1\leq k\leq 9}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|},\quad\bm{\Gamma}(\rho)_{0}\lesssim 1.

6
Elliptic estimates

In this section we estimate the highest (11th order) derivative of
connection coefficients. These results are needed in the energy
estimates of the auxiliary fields
\varphi_{0,1,2}
. The reason one
cannot directly use the transport estimate is due to the presence of
angular derivatives and Weyl curvature terms leading to even higher
derivatives of said quantities. To overcome this problem, based on the
analysis on topological 2-spheres
\mathcal{S}
, as introduced in
Chapter 2 of
[
8
]
, which provided an estimate of up to nine
derivatives of the Gaussian curvature on
\mathcal{S}
, we can obtain
an estimate for the highest derivative of the connection coefficients
from the information of the divergence and curl of its’ lower
derivatives. The required Gaussian curvature estimates can be obtained
from the Codazzi equation. Further discussion on this strategy can be
found in
[
10
]
,
[
29
]
.

In this article we can simplify the discussion by making use of the
T-weight formalism and relying on
3.3.2
—which
shows that the divergence has the same information as the
curl. Additional simplifications are obtained by constructing some new
quantities via divergences and the Weyl curvature so that the
transport equation of these new quantities no longer involve the Weyl
tensor.

The connection coefficients can be divided into three categories
depending on the specific structure of their equations:

(i)

The first category contains
(\rho,\sigma)
,

(\mu,\lambda)
. For these coefficients we make use one transport
equation for
\rho
(
\mu
) which does not contain spherical
derivative and the Weyl tensor to obtain estimates. The leading
term in this estimate is
\sigma
(
\lambda
) which be controlled by
its divergence via the Codazzi equation and
Proposition
18
. Details are given in
Propositions
20
and
24
.

(ii)

The second category contains
\tau
and
\pi
. Their
transport equations do not contain angular derivative but have
components of the Weyl tensor. To avoid the eleventh derivative of
the Weyl, we construct new quantities with the divergence and Weyl
tensor for which we estimate up to the tenth derivative.
Proposition
18
then allows us to control the
top derivatives of
\tau
and
\pi
. This discussion is presented in
Propositions
21
and
22
.

(iii)

The third category consists of
{\underline{\omega}}
which is a pure
scalar with zero T-weight. This property leads to a relatively
complex construction. Via an auxiliary field, we construct the new
quantity
\tilde{\underline{\omega}}
to avoid the analysis of the eleventh
derivative of the Weyl tensor. More details are given in
Proposition
23
.

6.1
Preliminary elliptic estimates

Making use of the
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
estimates, we have the estimate:

Lemma 7
.

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{7}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||_{L_{sc}^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{9}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}K||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

The Gauss curvature satisfies the formula

\displaystyle K=2\mu\rho-\lambda\sigma-\bar{\lambda}\bar{\sigma}+6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}-\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}.

For
1\leq k\leq 9
we have that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}K||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\rho(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\mu||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\sigma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\varphi_{1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\varphi_{1}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{2}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\rho||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\mu||

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\sigma||+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\frac{1}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\varphi_{1}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\varphi_{1}||.

Then, applying the results in Corollary
1
we find
that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}K||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

When
k=0
, we have the troublesome term
\rho\mu
. To estimate
\mu

we make use of the definition of
{\tilde{\mu}}
to yield

\displaystyle||\mu||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq||{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||\frac{1}{|u|}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{|u|}{a}+1.

Hence, we have that

\displaystyle||K||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}1\times\frac{|u|}{a}+1\lesssim 1.

Then we have when
0\leq k\leq 9
that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}K||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Finally, making use of the Sobolev embedding theorem we obtain the
desired result.
∎

Remark 15
.

From the last lemma it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{7}|u|^{2}||(|u|^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}K)||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\leq 1,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{9}|u|||(|u|^{i}\mathcal{D}^{i}K)||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\leq 1.

With this result we have, in turn, that

\displaystyle|||u|^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq k-1}\left(|||u|^{i+1}\mathcal{D}^{j}\mathscr{D}_{f}||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+|||u|^{j}\mathcal{D}^{i}f||^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right),

where
\mathscr{D}_{f}
is the divergence related scalar defined
in (
24
). In terms of scale-invariant norms we have:

Proposition 18
.

Let
f
denote a quantity in Table
1
with nonzero
T-weight. Then under the bootstrap assumption, for
0\leq k\leq 11
,
one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq k-1}\left(||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i+1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\mathscr{D}_{f}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}f||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right).

The proof can be found in Sec 6.1 in Paper
[
29
]
. When
f

is a pure scalar, we have the following:

Proposition 19
.

Let
f
denote a quantity in Table
1
with zero
T-weight. Then, under the bootstrap assumption,
for
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1}f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k+1}\mathcal{D}^{k-1}({\bm{\Delta}}f)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}f||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S})},

where
{\bm{\Delta}}f\equiv 2\mathchar 1264\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}f
.

6.2
Elliptic estimates for the highest (11th) derivative of
the connection

The norm of the eleventh derivative quantities of the connection
coefficients is defined as

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}_{11}(u,v)

\displaystyle\equiv||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle+\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

In the following estimates, we make use of the
L^{2}(\mathcal{S})

estimates and the bootstrap assumptions

\displaystyle\bm{\varphi},\bm{\Psi},\bm{\Gamma}_{11}\leq\mathcal{O}.

We will show that auxiliary bootstrap assumption on
\bm{\Gamma}_{11}

can be improved to

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}_{11}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}^{2}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}^{2}+\bm{\varphi}+1.

Proposition 20
.

One has that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1

and

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

Proof.

Step 1
: Estimate of
\rho
.

We make use of structure equation for
\rho
—namely, equation
(
28a
):

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\rho

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}+\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
, one has that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho

\displaystyle=\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\sigma+\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=11,i_{3}+i_{4}<11}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}[\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma),\varphi_{0}]\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}[\Gamma(\rho,\sigma),\varphi_{0}].

Then, making use of the transport estimate in scale-invariant norm,
one has that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{5}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\rho\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=11,i_{3}+i_{4}<11}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}[\Gamma,\varphi_{0}]\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}[\Gamma,\varphi_{0}]||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=I_{1}+...+I_{4}.

For
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\rho||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

This term can be absorbed by the Grönwall inequality.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\sigma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

For
I_{3}
we have

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\varphi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]\leq\frac{a}{|u|}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1)\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]\leq\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}].

For
I_{4}
we have

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=11}\int_{0}^{v}\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}[\Gamma,\varphi_{0}]||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}[\Gamma,\varphi_{0}]||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}+1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

Collecting the results above we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}].

Following the same strategy one can estimate the rest of the strings
of derivatives in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}{\tilde{\tau}}
and obtain the same
result. That leads to

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}].

(40)

Step 2
: Elliptic estimates for
\sigma
.

For
\sigma
we apply the structure
equation (
28m
) —namely

\displaystyle\mathchar 1264\relax\rho-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\sigma

\displaystyle=\tau\rho-\bar{\tau}\sigma-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}.

As the T-weight of
\sigma
is
-2
, hence we have that

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{\sigma}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\sigma=\mathchar 1264\relax\rho-\tau\rho+\bar{\tau}\sigma+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}.

Now, applying Proposition
18
, we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 10}(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mathscr{D}_{\sigma}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1,

where in the second step we have made use of

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

for
i\leq 10
.

Then using Grönwall’s inequality we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1,

(41)

so that integrating along the outgoing lightcone we find that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

Substituting (
41
) back into (
40
)
we see that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

Integrating along the outgoing lightcone we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1.

∎

Next, we have that

Proposition 21
.

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

We define the field

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tau}}\equiv\mathscr{D}_{\tau}-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\tau-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}.

with zero T-weight and signature
1
. The strategy of the proof is to
use of the estimates up to the tenth derivative of
{\tilde{\tau}}
to obtain
the estimate of the eleventh derivative of
\tau
via
Proposition
18
. Making use of the structure
equation of
\tau
, equation (
28i
),
and the Bianchi identity for
\mathchar 1278\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}
,
equation (
29c
), we find the following
equation for
{\tilde{\tau}}
:

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\tilde{\tau}}

\displaystyle=6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}+\tau\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\sigma}+\bar{\sigma}\mathchar 1264\relax\tau+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\pi+\pi\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\sigma+\rho\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\pi}+\bar{\pi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\rho+\bar{\tau}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\sigma+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\tau}

\displaystyle+2\rho{\tilde{\tau}}+\lambda\Psi_{0}-\pi{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}-\rho{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}-3\mu\varphi_{0}^{2}-3\bar{\sigma}\varphi_{1}^{2}+\pi\bar{\pi}\rho+\pi^{2}\sigma+\bar{\pi}\bar{\sigma}\tau-\bar{\sigma}\tau^{2}-\bar{\pi}\rho\bar{\tau}-\sigma\bar{\tau}^{2}.

Remarkably, we see that this equation does not involve derivatives of
the Weyl tensor. Hence we can avoid the estimate of the eleventh
derivative of Weyl. We now first estimate the
k=10
derivative
of
{\tilde{\tau}}
. For this, we first
estimate
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}
. Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
one has
that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}

\displaystyle=\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\varphi_{1}+\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)+\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)+\Gamma(\lambda,\pi,\rho)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\bm{\Psi}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}\neq k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}\neq k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}\leq k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\bm{\Psi}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\mu,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\varphi\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\varphi+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\Gamma

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\tau}},

where
\Gamma
contains
\rho
,
\sigma
,
\pi
,
\tau
,
\varphi

contain
\varphi_{0}
and
\varphi_{1}
. Then, making use of the
transport estimate in scale-invariant norm, one finds that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1278\relax(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{10}\bm{\Psi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}\neq k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}\neq k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}\leq k-1}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\bm{\Psi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\mu,\sigma)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\varphi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\lesssim 1+I_{1}+...+I_{9}.

For
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\varphi_{0}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||\leq\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]\lesssim 1.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1.

This term can be handled using the Grönwall inequality. For
I_{3}
we
have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\Gamma(\lambda,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{10}\mathcal{D}^{10}\bm{\Psi}(\Psi_{0},...)||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}\}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\leq\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1.

For
I_{4}
we have

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

For
I_{5}
we have

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

For
I_{6}
we have

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\bm{\Psi}(\Psi_{0})||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

For
I_{7}
, the worst term
is
\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{0}
. In this case we follow
the same strategy used for
I_{51}
in Proposition
17
and
obtain that this term can be bounded by

\displaystyle I_{71}\leq\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

The rest of the terms in
I_{7}
are collectively denoted by

I_{72}
. We find that

\displaystyle I_{72}

\displaystyle\leq a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{j_{1}}\Gamma...\mathcal{D}^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},..)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\varphi||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\varphi||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{5}}\varphi||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|}{a}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

For
I_{8}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{8}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{5}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}+1}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

Finally, for
I_{9}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{9}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\tau}}||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}+1}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\leq 1.

Collect the results above one concludes that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Following the same strategy one can estimate the rest of the strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}{\tilde{\tau}}
and obtain the same result. That leads
to

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\tau}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Now, making use of the definition

{\tilde{\tau}}=\mathscr{D}_{\tau}-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\tau-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathscr{D}_{\tau}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Applying Proposition
18
, we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 10}(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mathscr{D}_{\tau}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

We now transform the estimate on
\mathcal{S}_{u,v}
to one on the
light cone so that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

and

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

A similar strategy is used to construct an estimate for the
coefficient
\pi
.

Proposition 22
.

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Proof.

In order to estimate the eleventh derivative of
\pi
we introduce
the following quantity:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\pi}}\equiv\mathscr{D}_{\pi}+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}=\mathchar 1264\relax\pi+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}.

The T-weight of
{\tilde{\pi}}
is zero and its signature of is
1
. Next, we
make use of the structure
equation (
28j
) and the Bianchi
identities (
29f
) to obtain the following
equation for
{\tilde{\pi}}
:

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}{\tilde{\pi}}+2\mu{\tilde{\pi}}

\displaystyle=-6\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}-(\bar{\pi}+\tau)\mathchar 1264\relax\lambda-\bar{\tau}\mathchar 1264\relax\mu-\lambda(\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\pi}+\mathchar 1264\relax\tau)-\mu\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\tau}-\bar{\lambda}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\pi-\pi\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\lambda}

\displaystyle-\tau{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-\mu{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\sigma\Psi_{4}-3\bar{\lambda}\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}-3\rho\varphi_{2}^{2}.

Observe that this equation does not involve derivatives of the Weyl
tensor —hence, we can avoid estimating its eleventh derivative. Now,
we first estimate the
k=10
derivative of
{\tilde{\pi}}
by first looking at

\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}
. Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
one has that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}+(k+2)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}=\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\varphi_{1}+\Gamma(\mu,\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\tau+\lambda\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\pi+\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\Gamma(\tau,\mu,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\bm{\Psi}({\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4})+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\lambda,\rho)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}(1,2)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\lambda,\mu)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\lambda,\mu)+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}{\tilde{\pi}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,{\tilde{\mu}},\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-1}\bm{\Psi}.

Denote the right-hand side of the last equation by
F
. One can verify
that

\displaystyle s_{2}({\tilde{\pi}})=1,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\pi}})=\frac{k+2}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+4}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+2),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k+1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\pi}})=-1.

Now, making use of Proposition
4
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

For convenience define

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

Substituting into the expression of
F
one has that

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{10}\bm{\Psi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\lambda,\rho)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma(\mu,..)\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},..)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}{\tilde{\pi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},..)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\bm{\Psi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}=I_{1}+...+I_{8}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma(\varphi_{2})_{0,\infty}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle=\Gamma(\varphi_{2})_{0,\infty}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\Gamma(\varphi_{2})_{0,\infty}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a\Gamma(\varphi_{2})_{0,\infty}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Gamma(\varphi_{2})_{0,\infty}\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]\leq 1.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\mu||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\lambda||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\lambda||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\mu)_{0,\infty}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)_{0,\infty}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)_{0,\infty}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\mu)_{0,\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}(\tau)+\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)_{0,\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}(\tau)+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}(\mu)+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}(\lambda)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}(\tau)+1.

The integral in the last line can be absorbed using the Grönwall
inequality. For
I_{3}
, we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\mu||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\tau||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||\sigma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\Psi_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\mu)_{0,\infty}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau)_{0,\infty}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{4}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\mu)_{0,\infty}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau)_{0,\infty}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{4}]\leq\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+1.

For
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\varphi_{2}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{5}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\lambda,\rho)||\times||\bm{\varphi}(1,2)||\times||\bm{\varphi}||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{5}}(\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{3})\leq 1.

For
I_{6}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,..)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1-i}\Gamma||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{7}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}{\tilde{\pi}}||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{8}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{8}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\Gamma||\times||\Psi||\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

Collecting the results above we can conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}(\tau)+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

This inequality leads to

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\pi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

By the definition of

{\tilde{\pi}}=\mathscr{D}_{\pi}+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}=\mathchar 1264\relax\pi+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathscr{D}_{\pi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Now applying Proposition
18
, we see that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 10}(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mathscr{D}_{\pi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

Then, integrating along the outgoing lightcone we finally conclude
that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1.

∎

Proposition 23
.

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1.

Proof.

We estimate
{\underline{\omega}}
in three steps.

Step 1
: Construct a new scalar
\tilde{\underline{\omega}}
.

First we construct an auxiliary function
{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}}
with zero
T-weight through the relation

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}}=-i(\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2})

(42)

with trivial initial data on
\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{\star}
. Here
{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}}
is real.
First we apply
\mathchar 1264\relax
and use the commutator relation to find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax(i\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})=\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}-\mathchar 1264\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+i\rho\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}}+i\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}}+(\bar{\pi}-\tau)(\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}).

(43)

Then, making use of the structure equation for
{\underline{\omega}}
,
(
28k
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\underline{\omega}}=2\tau\bar{\tau}+2\tau\pi+2\bar{\tau}\bar{\pi}+{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2},

and then we have

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}=

\displaystyle\mathchar 1264\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\rho\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle+[\mathchar 1264\relax+(\bar{\pi}-\tau)](2\tau\bar{\tau}+2\tau\pi+2\bar{\tau}\bar{\pi}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}).

(44)

Together with (
43
) we see that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax[\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}+i\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}}]=

\displaystyle 2\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\rho(\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}+i\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})+\sigma(\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}{\underline{\omega}}+i\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax\Gamma+\Gamma^{3}+\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax\bm{\varphi}+\Gamma\bm{\varphi}^{2}+\Gamma{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},

(45)

where in the previous equation
\Gamma
contains
\tau
and
\pi
,
while
\bm{\varphi}
contains
\varphi_{0}
and
\varphi_{2}
. Then,
making use of the Bianchi identity (
29e
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}=

\displaystyle-6\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}-2\lambda{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+3\pi{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+2\rho{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}

\displaystyle-3\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}\bar{\pi}+6\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mu-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\lambda-6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\pi,

we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax[\mathchar 1264\relax({\underline{\omega}}+i{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})-2\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]=

\displaystyle\rho[\mathchar 1264\relax({\underline{\omega}}+i{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})-2\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}({\underline{\omega}}+i{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax\Gamma+\Gamma^{3}+\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax\bm{\varphi}+\Gamma\bm{\varphi}^{2}+\Gamma{\tilde{\Psi}}.

In the previous equation
\Gamma
contains
\tau
,
\pi
,
\mu

and
\lambda
;
\bm{\varphi}
contains
\varphi_{0}
,
\varphi_{1}
and

\varphi_{2}
; and
{\tilde{\Psi}}
contains
{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}
,
{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}

and
{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}
. Crucially, there is no derivative of curvature on the
right-hand side.

Now, we define

\displaystyle\tilde{\underline{\omega}}\equiv\mathchar 1264\relax({\underline{\omega}}+i{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})-2\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},

and find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\tilde{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle=\rho\tilde{\underline{\omega}}-\sigma\tilde{\underline{\omega}}^{*}+2\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}{\underline{\omega}}+2\tau(\mathchar 1264\relax\pi+\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\tau})+2\bar{\tau}(\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\pi}+\mathchar 1264\relax\tau)+2\pi\mathchar 1264\relax\tau+2\bar{\pi}\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\tau}+6\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{0}+6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{2}

\displaystyle+12\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}+12\bar{\lambda}\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-12\mu\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}+6\pi\varphi_{1}^{2}+6\bar{\pi}\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+12\bar{\pi}\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-6\tau\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+4\bar{\lambda}\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}-4\bar{\pi}\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}

\displaystyle-2\rho\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-2\sigma{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}-2\tau\bar{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+2\pi\bar{\pi}\tau-2\pi\tau^{2}+2\bar{\pi}^{2}\bar{\tau}-2\tau^{2}\bar{\tau}.

The key observation is that this equation does not contain derivatives
of the Weyl curvature. Hence we can avoid estimating the eleventh
derivative of the Weyl tensor. The T-weight of
\tilde{\underline{\omega}}

is
-1
and its signature is
\frac{3}{2}
.

Step 2
: Estimate of the
k=10
derivative
of
\tilde{\underline{\omega}}
.

Commuting
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax
one has that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle=\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\bm{\varphi}+\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\lambda,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}+\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)+\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\sigma,{\underline{\omega}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\lambda,\rho)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\Psi}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\mu,\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}.

Now, we make use of the transport estimate in scale-invariant norm, to
find that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1278\relax(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{11}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\sigma\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\Psi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+I_{1}+...+I_{11}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}a^{5}(||\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}a^{5}||\varphi_{0}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}a^{5}||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}a^{5}||\varphi_{1}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a}.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a^{5}}{|u|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a}\left(\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\Gamma||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\right)\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a}\leq 1,

where in the previous expression
\Gamma
contains
\tau

and
\pi
. For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}\frac{|u|\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}||_{L_{sc}^{2}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}\leq 1,

where
\Gamma
contains
\tau
,
\pi
,
\rho
,
\sigma

and
\lambda
. For
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

which can be absorbed making use of the Grönwall inequality. In the
last expression
\bm{\Gamma}
is meant to contain
\rho

and
\sigma
. For
I_{5}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}||\sigma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}.

For
I_{6}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

For
I_{7}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

For
I_{8}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{8}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\bm{\Psi}||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}-1}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}-2}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{1}{a|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq 1.

For
I_{9}
the worst behaved terms
contain
\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{j}\varphi_{1}
. It follows then that

\displaystyle I_{9}

\displaystyle\leq a^{5}||\mathcal{D}^{j_{1}}\Gamma...\mathcal{D}^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\mu,..)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\varphi||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\varphi||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{5}}\varphi||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u|}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a}\mathcal{O}=\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0}){\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})+\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{a^{\frac{3}{2}}|u|^{i_{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}\leq 1.

For
I_{10}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{10}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{5}}\Gamma||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+3}\leq\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{3}\leq 1.

Finally, for
I_{11}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{11}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}=\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}+1}{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

Combining the above estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{11}
we have that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+1.

Following the same strategy one can estimate the remaining strings of
derivatives in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}
. This yields the
same result. Hence, one concludes that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+1.

Step 3
: Make use of the elliptic estimate.

The estimate of the tenth derivative of
\tilde{\underline{\omega}}
implies an
estimate for the ninth derivative of
\bm{\Delta}{\underline{\omega}}
, where

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}{\underline{\omega}}\equiv/\kern-5.80002pt\nabla_{a}/\kern-5.80002pt\nabla^{a}{\underline{\omega}}=2\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}.

Making use of Proposition
19
we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{9}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{9}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}+i\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1.

Integrating along the ingoing lightcone we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1.

∎

Proposition 24
.

We have that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

and that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Step 1
: Estimate of
{\tilde{\mu}}
,
\mu
.

We make use of

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}{\tilde{\mu}}+2\mu{\tilde{\mu}}={\tilde{\mu}}^{2}-{\underline{\omega}}\mu-\lambda\bar{\lambda}-2\varphi_{2}^{2}

and then commute
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
with
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
for
k=11
. We find that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}+(k+2)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}=

\displaystyle\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}+\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\lambda+\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}{\underline{\omega}}+{\underline{\omega}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}+\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}\varphi_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\varphi_{2}.

Denote the right-hand side of the previous expression by
F
. It
follows then that

\displaystyle s_{2}({\tilde{\mu}})=1,\ \ s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}})=\frac{k+2}{2},\ \ s_{2}(F)=\frac{k+4}{2},

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}=-(k+2),\ \ \lambda_{1}=-\lambda_{0}-1=k+1,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(F)=-3,\ \ \lambda_{1}-2s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\mu}})=-1.

Now, we make use of Proposition
4
, so as to
obtain

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

Multiplying by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
one concludes that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}F||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mu\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda,{\underline{\omega}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\lesssim 1+I_{1}+...+I_{6}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

This term can be absorbed using the Grönwall inequality. For
I_{2}

we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\Gamma||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}
