\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\mu||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}.

For
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||\varphi_{2}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}={\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\leq{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\varphi_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}].

For
I_{5}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\Gamma||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}=\mathcal{O}^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

For
I_{6}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\varphi_{2}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\varphi_{2}||

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|\mathcal{O}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}=\mathcal{O}^{2}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 1.

Combining the estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{6}
we have that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathchar 1264\relax^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+1.

Following the same strategy one can estimate the remaining strings of
derivatives in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\tilde{\underline{\omega}}
and obtain the same
result. This leads us to conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+1.

(46)

Step 2
: Elliptic estimates of
\lambda
.

To estimate
\lambda
we make use of the structure equation
(
28n
) —namely

\displaystyle\mathchar 1264\relax\lambda-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mu=\bar{\tau}\mu-\tau\lambda-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}.

As the T-weight of
\lambda
is
2
, it follows
that
\mathscr{D}_{\lambda}=\mathchar 1264\relax\lambda
. Accordingly, the above
structure equation can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{\lambda}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mu+\bar{\tau}\mu-\tau\lambda-{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}.

Now, applying Proposition
18
, we have that

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 10}(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mathscr{D}_{\lambda}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mu(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1.

Hence, integrating along the ingoing lightcone we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\sum_{i=0}^{10}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{10}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\mu(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{10}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\lambda(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1

\displaystyle=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+1.

Now, for the second integral in the last inequality we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{10}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{10}\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq 1.

For the third and fourth integrals we see that

\displaystyle I_{2}+I_{3}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}\leq 1.

Hence, we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1.

(47)

Substituting (
46
) and apply Grönwall’s inequality we
have that

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+1

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bm{\varphi}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1,

(48)

so that integrating along the ingoing lightcone we end up with

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\varphi}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1.

Now, we observe that for spherical derivatives, the norm of
\mu

coincides with that of
{\tilde{\mu}}
. Hence, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\bm{\varphi}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1.

Accordingly, observing (
47
), we see that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim 1.

Moreover, from (
6.2
) we have that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim(\bm{\varphi}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2}.

Following the same strategy as in the previous paragraphs we finally
conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{10}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+1

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a}(\bm{\varphi}+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}(\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]^{2}+1)

\displaystyle\leq 1.

∎

6.3
Summary of the analysis of the top derivative of the
connection

In this section we have made use of the results in Section
5
and the bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}_{11},\bm{\varphi},\bm{\Psi}\leq\mathcal{O},

to conclude with the estimates

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\sigma||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\tau||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\pi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]+1,

\displaystyle||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{5}\mathcal{D}^{11}\lambda||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1.

So, in general we have that

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}_{11}\lesssim\bm{\Psi}^{2}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}^{2}+\bm{\varphi}+1.

7
Energy estimates

In this section we discuss the construction of energy estimates for
the components of the Weyl tensor and for the auxiliary
fields
\varphi_{0,1,2}
. Making use of the bootstrap
assumption (
36
) and the the results in previous sections
we will show that

\displaystyle\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}+1.

In Section
7.1
we introduce our main
tool in the analysis —namely, the energy inequality. Due to the
central role played by the connection coefficient
\mu
, which are
large, we follow the strategy in An’s work
[
28
]
to manage the
terms appearing in the principal part with an explicit
\mu
, and thus
obtain an improved energy inequality for the auxiliary fields and the
Weyl curvature. The latter will also be a key tool in the
analysis. For the pairs
(\Psi_{i},\Psi_{i+1})
where
i=0,...,3

and
(\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1})
, this inequality can be used to manage the
terms involving
\mu
. Now, for the pair
(\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2})
, the
term
\mu\varphi_{1}
in (
27c
) leads to a
significant difference in the energy inequality:

\displaystyle||\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim....

and
\mu\varphi_{0}
in (
27d
) is a large
term. As a result, the estimate for
(\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2})
is
comparatively coarse, and so not good enough for the purposes of
studying the formation of trapped surfaces.

Another problem in the energy estimates comes from the Bianchi
equation (
29h
). According to the
bootstrap assumption, the terms
\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax’\varphi_{2}

and
\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mu
are large, and lead to a logarithmic
contribution in the integral over
D_{u,v}
. This behaviour can also
obstruct the proof.

To address these problems we introduce an auxiliary field

\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{2}\equiv\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{2}+\mu\bar{\varphi}_{1}.

The first advantage of this definition is that the structure of
equations of pair
(\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1},\tilde{\varphi}_{2})
have the same

good structure
of the other pairs and is also consistent with
the signature
s_{2}
. For this auxiliary variable the energy inequality
leads to an estimate of exactly the same type as the other pairs. The
second advantage is that the leading terms of
\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{2}

and
\mu\bar{\varphi}_{1}
, which are large terms, but cancel exactly to
leading order because of our choice of initial data combined with the
fact that they appear in the field equations with the desired
coefficients (see remark
11
for more
detail). This implies that
\tilde{\varphi}_{2}
is in fact not a leading
order term and, hence, the estimate of
(\Psi_{3},\Psi_{4})
goes through
undamaged. The details of this heuristic discussion are provided in
Propositions
27
and
29
.

7.1
Preliminary energy estimates

We begin by recalling integration by parts on causal diamonds. Making
use of (
22
) and (
23
)
one has the following:

Lemma 8

(
integral over causal diamonds of derivatives of
a scalar
)
.

Let
f
be a scalar with zero T-weight
in the causal diamond
\mathcal{D}_{u,v}
. One has then that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}\Delta f=\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}f-\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}f+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}({\underline{\omega}}-2\mu)f,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}Df=\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}f-\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}f+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}2\rho f.

Corollary 2
.

Let
f=f_{1}f_{2}
and assume
s(f)=0
, then

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}f_{1}\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}f_{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}f_{2}\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}f_{1}=\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}f_{1}f_{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}f_{1}f_{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}({\underline{\omega}}-2\mu)f_{1}f_{2},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}f_{1}\mathchar 1278\relax f_{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}f_{2}\mathchar 1278\relax f_{1}=\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}f_{1}f_{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}f_{1}f_{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}2\rho f_{1}f_{2}.

Proposition 25
.

Let
\phi
be either a T-weighted quantity or a derivative of
one. Moreover, let
\lambda_{1}=\lambda_{0}-1
. Then

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\phi,\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi+\lambda_{0}\mu\phi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\phi|^{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\phi|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}h|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\phi|^{2},

where
h
obeys the bound

\displaystyle|h|\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}},

where

\displaystyle 2\langle\phi,\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi+2\lambda_{0}\mu\phi\rangle=\bar{\phi}(\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi+\lambda_{0}\mu\phi)+\phi(\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\bar{\phi}+\lambda_{0}\mu\bar{\phi}).

Proof.

Let
f\equiv Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\phi|^{2}
. Making use
of
23
one then has that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}u}\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\phi|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}2Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\phi,\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\phi+\lambda_{0}\mu\phi\rangle+\int_{\mathcal{S}_{u,v}}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\bigg(2\mu(1-\lambda_{0})-{\underline{\omega}}-\frac{2\lambda_{1}}{|u|}\bigg),

where we have made use of the fact that
\Delta Q={\underline{\omega}}Q
. Now, let

\displaystyle h\equiv-2\mu(1-\lambda_{0})+{\underline{\omega}}+\frac{2\lambda_{1}}{|u|}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle h=2\lambda_{1}(\mu-\frac{1}{u})+{\underline{\omega}}.

Making use of the bootstrap assumption one concludes
that
|h|\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}}
. Now, integrating with
respect to
\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v
and applying the
Fundamental
Theorem of Calculus
with respect to
u
we obtain the desired
result.
∎

In the following, let

\displaystyle(\Psi_{I},\Psi_{II})\in\{(\Psi_{0},\tilde{\Psi}_{1}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{1},\tilde{\Psi}_{2}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{2},\tilde{\Psi}_{3}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{3},\Psi_{4}),(\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1}),(\varphi_{1},\varphi_{2})\}.

Using this notation, the Bianchi identities and the equations for the
scalar field take the schematic form

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{I}+\lambda_{0}\mu\Psi_{I}-\mathchar 1264\relax\Psi_{II}=P_{0},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\Psi_{II}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{I}=Q_{0}.

Applying the derivative
\mathchar 1264\relax
and the commutator relations it
follows that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}+(\lambda_{0}+k)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\Psi_{II}

\displaystyle=P_{k},

(49a)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}

\displaystyle=Q_{k},

(49b)

where

\displaystyle P_{k}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}P_{0}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Psi_{I}

and

\displaystyle Q_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Psi_{I}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}Q_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Psi_{II}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}K\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\Psi_{I}.

The signature of the various terms involved satisfy

\displaystyle s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I})=\frac{k}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{I}),\quad

\displaystyle s_{2}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{2})=\frac{k+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{I}),

\displaystyle s_{2}(P_{k})=\frac{k+2}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{I}),\quad

\displaystyle s_{2}(Q_{k})=\frac{k+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{I}).

Now applying the expressions in Proposition

25
to
\Psi_{I}
one has that

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I},\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}+(\lambda_{0}+k)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}h|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2},

where
\lambda_{1}=\lambda_{0}+k-1
. Applying
Corollary
2

to
f_{1}=\bar{f}_{2}=|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}
it follows that

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II},\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}2\rho|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2},

so that combine the above two equalities one ends up with

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I},\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}+(\lambda_{0}+k)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}\rangle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II},\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}-\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}h|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}2\rho|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}.

Now, making use of equations (
49a
), (
49b
)
in the left-hand side of the previous expression and making use of
integration by parts (Lemma
5
), we have
that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I},P_{k}\rangle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II},Q_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}h|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}-\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}2\rho|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}.

From the bootstrap assumption we have that

\displaystyle|h|\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|^{2}},\quad|\rho|\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}.

Letting

\displaystyle f(u)

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2},\quad g(v)\equiv\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2},

\displaystyle X(u,v)

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I},P_{k}\rangle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II},Q_{k}\rangle,

one has that

\displaystyle f(u)+g(v)\leq X(u,v)+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}f(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u+\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}g(v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v.

Hence, we find that

\displaystyle g(v)\leq X(u,v)+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}f(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u+\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u|}g(v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v.

Applying the Grönwall-type estimate, we thus obtain that

\displaystyle g(v)\lesssim X(u,v)+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}f(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u,

and, moreover, that

\displaystyle f(u)\lesssim X(u,v)+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}f(u^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u.

Applying the Grönwall-type estimate again, one has

\displaystyle f(u)\lesssim X(u,v).

In other words, one has that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I},P_{k}\rangle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II},Q_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v)}Q^{-1}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}|^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||P_{k}|+2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{u,v}}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||Q_{k}|.

Now, recalling the estimate stating
that
||Q,Q^{-1}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}
are not far
from
1
, we can neglect term
Q^{-1}
in the above inequality. Then,
multiplying by
a^{-k-2s_{2}(\Psi_{I})}
on both sides and making use of
the definitions

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\equiv a^{-s_{2}(\phi)}|u|^{2s_{2}(\phi)}||\phi||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

\displaystyle||\phi||_{L^{1}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}

\displaystyle\equiv a^{-s_{2}(\phi)}|u|^{2s_{2}(\phi)-1}||\phi||_{L^{1}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

we can rewrite the inequality as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}|u|^{2\lambda_{1}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2k}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2k-2}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2k}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2k-2}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},0})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2k-1}|||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||P_{k}|||_{L^{1}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{1}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2k-1}|||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||Q_{k}|||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Now, applying the Hölder inequality in scale-invariant norm and
observing that
\lambda_{1}=\lambda_{0}+k-1
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}|u|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-4}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-2}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-4}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},0})}

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-4}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a|u^{\prime}|^{2\lambda_{0}-4s_{2}(\Psi_{I})-4}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

For
(\Psi_{I},\Psi_{II})\in\{(\Psi_{0},\tilde{\Psi}_{1}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{1},\tilde{\Psi}_{2}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{2},\tilde{\Psi}_{3}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{3},\Psi_{4}),(\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1})\}
, the Bianchi identities and
the equations for the scalar field satisfy that
\lambda_{0}=2s_{2}(\Psi_{I})+1
. Consequently,
the above inequality takes the form

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},0})}

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Finally, recalling the definitions of the norms on the lightcone
—namely,

\displaystyle||\phi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{0}^{v}||\phi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime},

\displaystyle||\phi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\phi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime},

we conclude that

\displaystyle||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

7.2
Energy estimates for the auxiliary field

Proposition 26
.

For
0\leq k\leq 11
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

When
(\Psi_{I},\Psi_{II})=(\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1})
, we make use of
the equations (
27a
)
and (
27a
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{0}-\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}

\displaystyle=({\underline{\omega}}-\mu)\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}-\bar{\tau}\varphi_{1}-\tau\bar{\varphi}_{1},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle=(\pi-\bar{\tau})\varphi_{0}+\bar{\sigma}\varphi_{1}+\rho\bar{\varphi}_{1}.

Now, let

\displaystyle P_{k}

\displaystyle\equiv\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}+\tau\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}+\rho\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{2}+\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}})+\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\tau+\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\rho

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\tau,\rho,{\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-1}\bm{\varphi}(\varphi_{0,1,2}).

\displaystyle Q_{k}

\displaystyle\equiv\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}+\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)+\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}+\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}(\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}K\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\varphi_{0}.

Making use of the energy inequality we have that

\displaystyle||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

so that multiplying both sides by
a^{\frac{2k-2}{2}}
on both hands
side we find that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+M+N,

where

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Finally, multiplying by
a^{-1}
we are led to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+\frac{1}{a}(M+N).

(50)

From here one the proof requires a number of intermediate steps.

Step 1
: Estimate
M
.

We have that

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=2\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]J^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Now, substituting the definition of
P_{k}
into
J
we have that

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\tau\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\rho\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\tau||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{k}\rho||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma(\lambda,...)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{7}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}({\bm{\Gamma}}({\underline{\omega}})_{0,\infty}^{2}+\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)_{0,\infty}^{2})||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\tau||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

For

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\rho||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}^{2}||\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\frac{1}{a}\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]^{2}.

For

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\underline{\omega}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

For

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\tau||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

For

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}a\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

Combining the estimates for

we conclude that

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{a}

and then

Step 2
: Estimate

.

We have

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=2\left(\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}H^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]H^{\frac{1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Q_{k}
into

\displaystyle H\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\pi,\tau)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}K\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{6}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}(\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}({\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}^{2}+a{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}^{2})||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}^{2}\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}(\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+1)^{2}.

For
I_{3}
and
I_{4}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{3}+I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{0,1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0},\varphi_{1})_{0,\infty}^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}(\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]+\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+1)^{2}.

For
I_{5}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k-1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{j_{1}}\Gamma...\mathcal{D}^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}||\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O})^{2}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{i_{2}+2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{2(i_{2}+2)}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}.

For
I_{6}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}K||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}\varphi_{0}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}.

Finally, combining the estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{6}
we conclude that

\displaystyle H\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}(\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]+\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}]+1)^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{2}(\underline{\bm{\Psi}}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}+1)^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|},

and that

\displaystyle N\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}](\underline{\bm{\Psi}}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}+1).

Step 3
: Summary.

From the analysis in Steps 1 and 2 we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+\frac{1}{a}(M+N)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a}(\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}](\underline{\bm{\Psi}}+\bm{\Psi}+\bm{\varphi}+1)+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}+\mathcal{O}^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}])

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Following the same strategy one can estimate the remaining strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\varphi_{0}
and
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\varphi_{1}

and obtain the same result. We thus obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\leq\mathcal{I}^{2}_{0}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

∎

Proposition 27
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

Proof.

In this proof we make use of the fields
\tilde{\varphi}_{1}

and
\tilde{\varphi}_{2}
where

\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{1}\equiv\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1},\qquad\tilde{\varphi}_{2}\equiv\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{2}+\mu\bar{\varphi}_{1}.

Starting from the equations (
27c
)
and (
27d
) —i.e.

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{2}

\displaystyle=-\mu\varphi_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\varphi_{2}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle=-\mu\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}+\pi\varphi_{1}+\bar{\pi}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

we apply
\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}
and make use of the commutator relations so as to
obtain

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\tilde{\varphi}_{1}+3\mu\tilde{\varphi}_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax\tilde{\varphi}_{2}

\displaystyle=-\lambda\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{1}-\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax\lambda-3\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax\mu-\bar{\lambda}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\lambda},

(51)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\tilde{\varphi}_{2}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\tilde{\varphi}_{1}

\displaystyle=\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax\pi+\bar{\pi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+2\pi\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}-\bar{\tau}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}-\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mu+\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\pi+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\pi}+\varphi_{2}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\rho

\displaystyle+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\tilde{\varphi}_{2}+2\rho\tilde{\varphi}_{2}+\bar{\sigma}\bar{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}+\pi^{2}\varphi_{1}+2\pi\bar{\pi}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+\pi\rho\varphi_{2}+\lambda\sigma\bar{\varphi}_{1}

\displaystyle-\pi\bar{\tau}\varphi_{1}-\bar{\pi}\bar{\tau}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\rho\bar{\tau}\varphi_{2},

(52)

where we have made use
of
\bar{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}=\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}=\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}=\tilde{\varphi}_{1}

—i.e.
\tilde{\varphi}_{1}
is real. We can also use the same strategy
for the pair
(\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{2},\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{1})
as long as we
apply
\mathchar 1264\relax
to (
27c
)
and (
27d
). By definition,

s_{2}({\tilde{\varphi}}_{2})=3/2
,
s_{2}({\tilde{\varphi}}_{1})=1
. We now apply the energy
estimate to the pair
({\tilde{\varphi}}_{1},{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2})
.
Choosing
\lambda_{0}=3=2s_{2}({\tilde{\varphi}}_{1})+1
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},0})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

where
0\leq k\leq 10
. Multiplying by
a^{k}
we arrive to

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},0})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},0})}

\displaystyle\quad+M+N,

where

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},

and we define

\displaystyle P_{k}=\lambda\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\varphi_{1}+\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)

\displaystyle Q_{k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\Gamma(\pi,\mu,\rho)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma,\varphi_{1})\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\sigma,\tau,\pi,\rho)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}K\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}+1}\varphi_{1}.

The rest of the proof consists of three steps.

Step 1
: Estimate
M
.
We have that

\displaystyle M\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Denoting the second integral by
J
and substituting
P_{k}
we have
that

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{4}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\lambda||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]\leq 1.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}}{a}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{O}^{4}\leq 1.

For
I_{3}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\lambda)||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

Finally,
I_{4}
leads to the same estimate as for
I_{3}
. Combining the
estimates for
I_{1},\dots,I_{4}
we have
J\lesssim 1
.

Step 2
: Estimating
N
.

We have that

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Denoting the second integral by
H
and substituting
Q_{k}
we have that

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\pi,\mu,\rho)||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}(\sigma,...,\varphi_{1})\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\lambda,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}K\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{5}.

For
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}+2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\mathcal{O}^{2}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\leq 1.

For
I_{2}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\mu||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\pi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\rho||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u|}{a}\mathcal{O}+\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{2})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})^{2}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\mu||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\pi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})^{2}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\rho||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{|u|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{O}^{4}+\frac{1}{a|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4}+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{|u|}\leq 1.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4}\leq 1.

For
I_{4}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\frac{|u^{\prime}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4}\leq 1.

Finally, for
I_{5}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq 1.

Combine the above estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{5}
we have
H\lesssim 1
.

Step 3
: Summary for
0\leq k\leq 1
.

Collecting the estimates for
M
and
N
we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

Observing that the rest of the strings in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{1}}
give
rise to the same results, we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

We can also consider the pair
(\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{1},\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{2}+\mu\varphi_{1})
and obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}(\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{2}+\mu\varphi_{1})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

Thus, for
\varphi_{1}
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

Similarly, for
\varphi_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}(\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{2}+\mu\varphi_{1})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}(\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{2}+\mu\bar{\varphi}_{1})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}(\mu\varphi_{1})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\mu||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}{\tilde{\mu}}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{a^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}\lesssim 1.

Hence, one can conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v^{\prime}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

for
0\leq k\leq 10
.

The estimates of
\varphi_{1}
and
\varphi_{2}
on the light cone give
the same result. For the sake of conciseness we do not discuss the
details.
∎

7.3
Energy estimate for the Weyl curvature

Finally, in this section we obtain the energy estimates for the
components of the Weyl tensor.

Proposition 28
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

For the pair
(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1})
, we make use of the Bianchi
equations (
29b
)
and (
29a
)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{0}-\mathchar 1264\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}

\displaystyle=6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{1}+(2{\underline{\omega}}-\mu)\Psi_{0}-5\tau{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+3\sigma{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}

\displaystyle+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}\sigma-3\varphi_{0}^{2}\bar{\lambda}-3\varphi_{1}^{2}\rho-6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\sigma-12\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\tau,

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{0}=

\displaystyle-6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax\varphi_{0}+(\pi-2\bar{\tau})\Psi_{0}+4\rho{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}

\displaystyle+3\varphi_{0}^{2}(2\tau-\bar{\pi})+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\rho-6\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\sigma.

Applying
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
and commuting with
\mathchar 1278\relax
and
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}+(k+1)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}

\displaystyle=P_{k},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}

\displaystyle=Q_{k},

where

\displaystyle P_{k}

\displaystyle=\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\varphi_{1}+{\underline{\omega}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}+\tau\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma({\underline{\omega}},{\tilde{\mu}})\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Psi_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\lambda\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Psi_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma(\tau,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}({\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\rho,\sigma,\lambda,\tau)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi},

\displaystyle Q_{k}

\displaystyle=\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\varphi_{0}+\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{0}+\rho\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}(\Psi_{0},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi,\rho,\sigma)\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}.+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}K\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\Psi_{0}.

It follows then that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+M+N,

where

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}J^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

It is convenient to define

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})},\quad H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

The rest of the proof proceeds in three steps.

Step 1
: Estimate
J
.

Substituting
P_{k}
we have that

\displaystyle J
