\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}{\underline{\omega}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\tau\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\sigma\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma(\lambda,...)\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\lambda,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{7}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}.

For
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||{\underline{\omega}}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}({\underline{\omega}})_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}({\underline{\omega}})_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}({\underline{\omega}})_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}.

For
I_{3}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\tau||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau)_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]^{2}.

For
I_{4}
we observe that

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\sigma||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\sigma)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2}]^{2}.

For
I_{5}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\varphi_{0}||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1-i}\varphi_{1}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})^{2}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

For
I_{6}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma(\lambda,...)||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\Psi||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)^{2}a{\bm{\Gamma}}(\Psi_{0})^{2}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq\frac{a}{|u|}\leq 1.

Finally, for this term
I_{7}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\lambda,...)||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}a\mathcal{O}^{2}a\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

Collecting the estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{7}
we conclude that

\displaystyle J\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{4},

so that, in fact, one has that

\displaystyle M\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\mathcal{O}^{2}.

Step 2
: Estimate
H
.

Substituting
Q_{k}
in the definition for
H
we have that

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\rho\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{3}+i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\varphi\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\varphi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}K\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{7}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{3}}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}\leq\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}.

For
I_{2}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma(\tau,\pi)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\tau,\pi)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]^{2}.

For
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\rho||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}^{2}\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{k}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{1}]^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}^{2}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{1}]^{2}.

For
I_{4}
we have

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+...+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k+1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{j_{1}}\Gamma...\mathcal{D}^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\varphi_{0}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}+1}\varphi_{0}||\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O})^{2}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{i_{2}+2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2i_{2}+4}}\mathcal{O}^{2(i_{2}+2)}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\mathcal{O}^{4}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

For
I_{5}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\Psi||\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\mathcal{O}\right)^{2}\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{4}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{4}.

For
I_{6}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{1}}\Gamma||...||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{j_{i_{2}}}\Gamma||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\Gamma(\sigma,...)||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}||\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}||\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{|u|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}\right)^{2}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{i_{2}+3}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+4}}\mathcal{O}^{2i_{2}+6}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{4}}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\mathcal{O}^{6}\leq\frac{a^{4}}{|u|^{5}}\mathcal{O}^{6}.

Finally, for
I_{7}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}K||^{2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}\Psi_{0}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}a\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}^{2}.

Collecting the estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{7}
we obtain

\displaystyle H\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{O}^{6},

so that

\displaystyle N\leq a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}](\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{3}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}).

Step 3
: Summary.

From the estimate

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}+M+N,

multiplying by
\frac{1}{a}
we find that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}(M+N)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}](\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]+\frac{\mathcal{O}^{3}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}})

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Now, we observe that the rest of the strings
in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}\Psi_{0}
and
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}
give rise
to the same estimate. Hence, one concludes that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+\frac{1}{a}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}.

∎

Proposition 29
.

For
0\leq k\leq 10
, one has that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3}\}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\{{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2},{\tilde{\Psi}}_{3},\Psi_{4}\}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

Proof.

We use the notation
(\Psi_{I},\Psi_{II})
to denote any of the
pairs
\{(\tilde{\Psi}_{1},\tilde{\Psi}_{2}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{2},\tilde{\Psi}_{3}),(\tilde{\Psi}_{3},\Psi_{4})\}
. The Bianchi identities of
(\Psi_{I},\Psi_{II})
—i.e
(
29c
),
(
29d
),
(
29e
),
(
29f
),
(
29g
),
(
29h
)— have the structure

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{I}+(2s_{2}(\Psi_{I})+1)\mu\Psi_{I}-\mathchar 1264\relax\Psi_{II}

\displaystyle=\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax\bm{\varphi}+\Gamma\Psi+\Gamma\bm{\varphi}\bm{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\Psi_{II}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{I}

\displaystyle=\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax\bm{\varphi}+\Gamma\Psi+\Gamma\bm{\varphi}\bm{\varphi}.

Commuting
\mathchar 1278\relax
and
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
with
\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}
we have that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}+(2s_{2}(\Psi_{I})+k+1)\mu\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1}\Psi_{II}

\displaystyle=P_{k},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{II}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k}\Psi_{I}

\displaystyle=Q_{k},

where

\displaystyle P_{k}\equiv\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{k+1-i}\bm{\varphi}+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{k-i}\Psi+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}

\displaystyle Q_{k}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\Psi

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{1}}K\mathchar 1264\relax^{i_{2}}\Psi_{I}.

It follows then that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\lesssim||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{0}(u_{\infty},u))}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+M+N,

where we have defined

\displaystyle M\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

and

\displaystyle N\equiv 2\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

As in previous proofs, the argument proceeds in three steps.

Step 1
: Estimate for
M
.

One readily has that

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u^{\prime}}(0,v))}+H,

where

\displaystyle H\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}P_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Substituting the definition of
P_{k}
we have that

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=I_{1}+...+I_{4}.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{k+1-i}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{11}+I_{12}+I_{13},

so that for
I_{11}
we estimate

\displaystyle I_{11}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}\leq 1,

while for
I_{12}
one has that

\displaystyle I_{12}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\frac{1}{a}\underline{\bm{\varphi}}[\varphi_{2}]^{2}\leq 1,

and for
I_{13}
we have

\displaystyle I_{13}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k+1-i}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{|u|}\leq 1.

It follows from the above that
I_{1}\lesssim 1
.

Next, for the term
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{k-i}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=I_{21}+I_{22},

so that for
I_{21}
we have the estimate

\displaystyle I_{21}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},{\underline{\omega}})||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}},\sigma,{\underline{\omega}},\tau)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{2,3,4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}})\left(||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{2,3,4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}})\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{2,3,4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Gamma({\tilde{\mu}})}{a}(\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1}]^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{2,3},\Psi_{4}]^{2})\lesssim 1.

Moreover, for
I_{22}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{22}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i}\Gamma||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k-i}\Psi||^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a^{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}a\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{|u|}\leq 1.

Accordingly, we have that
I_{2}\lesssim 1
.

For the term
I_{3}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{1}}\Gamma||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{6}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\mathcal{O}^{2}=\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{\mathcal{O}^{6}}{a|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\leq 1.

For
I_{4}
we find that

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{1}}\varphi_{2}||^{2}\times||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{i_{2}}{\tilde{\varphi}}_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}^{2}\mathcal{O}^{2}\leq\frac{\mathcal{O}^{4}}{|u|}\leq 1.

Collecting the estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{4}
we have
that
H\lesssim 1
.

Step 2
: Estimate for
N

In this case one readily sees that

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{v}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{v^{\prime}}(u_{\infty},u))}+J,

where

\displaystyle J\equiv\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}Q_{k}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}.

Substituting the definition of
Q_{k}
we have that

\displaystyle J

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{5}}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=k-1}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}K\mathcal{D}^{i_{2}}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}=I_{1}+...+I_{4}.

We now proceed to estimate each of the terms
I_{1},\ldots,I_{4}
.

For the term
I_{1}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\varphi_{1}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\varphi_{2}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\bm{\varphi}\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}+1}\bm{\varphi}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=I_{11}+I_{12}+I_{13}+I_{14},

where for
I_{11}
one sees that

\displaystyle I_{11}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{0}]^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{12}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{12}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{1})^{2}_{0,\infty}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}\leq 1.

For
I_{13}
we see that

\displaystyle I_{13}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\varphi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})^{2}_{0,\infty}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k+1}\varphi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{2})^{2}_{0,\infty}\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]^{2}\leq 1,

while for
I_{14}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{14}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{O}^{2}+\mathcal{O}^{2}+\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{O}^{2})\right)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{|u|}\leq 1.

For the term
I_{2}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\lambda\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\Gamma(\lambda,...)\mathcal{D}^{k}({\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2,3},\Psi_{4})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=k,i_{4}<k}\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{1}}\Gamma(\tau,\pi)^{i_{2}}\mathcal{D}^{i_{3}}\Gamma\mathcal{D}^{i_{4}}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle=I_{21}+I_{22}+I_{23},

where for
I_{21}
one sees that

\displaystyle I_{21}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||\lambda||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)_{0,\infty}\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{0}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)_{0,\infty}\bm{\Psi}[\Psi_{0}]\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|}\leq 1.

Moreover, for
I_{22}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(||\Gamma(\lambda...)||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+||\rho||^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)^{2}_{0,\infty}\int_{0}^{v}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2,3}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}+\int_{0}^{v}\frac{{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)_{0,\infty}^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{k}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{k}\Psi_{4}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{S}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|u|}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)^{2}_{0,\infty}\bm{\Psi}[{\tilde{\Psi}}_{1,2,3}]^{2}+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\rho)^{2}_{0,\infty}\underline{\bm{\Psi}}[\Psi_{4}]^{2}\lesssim 1.

For
I_{23}
we have that

\displaystyle I_{23}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+1}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\Psi_{0})+{\bm{\Gamma}}(\Psi_{II}))\frac{|u|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\bm{\Gamma}}(\lambda)\right)^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|}\leq 1,

where we have made use of
{\bm{\Gamma}}(\Psi_{0},\lambda)\lesssim 1
.

For the term
I_{3}
one has that the worst term in
\Gamma\bm{\varphi}^{2}

is
\mu\varphi_{0}^{2}
. One then has that

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq\int_{0}^{v}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{\frac{i_{2}}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{i_{2}+2}}\mathcal{O}^{i_{2}}\frac{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}{a}\mathcal{O}a\mathcal{O}^{2}\right)^{2}\leq\frac{a}{|u|}\leq 1,

where we have made use of
{\bm{\Gamma}}(\mu)\lesssim 1

and
{\bm{\Gamma}}(\varphi_{0})\lesssim\bm{\varphi}[\varphi_{1}]+1\lesssim 1
.

I_{4}
: This integral can also be bounded by 1.

Collecting the estimates for
I_{1},\ldots,I_{4}
we conclude
that
J\lesssim 1
.

Summary.
From the estimates for
M
and
N
we have that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathchar 1264\relax)^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

Now, the rest of the strings of derivatives in
\mathcal{D}^{k_{i}}

give rise to the same result and hence we have that

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{I}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}_{u}(0,v))}+||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D})^{k}\Psi_{II}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(\mathcal{N}^{\prime}_{v}(u_{\infty},u))}\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{0}^{2}+1.

∎

Combining the estimates in last three sections we have improved the
bootstrap assumption

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+{\bm{\varphi}}\leq\mathcal{O}

by

\displaystyle{\bm{\Gamma}}+\bm{\Psi}+{\bm{\varphi}}\lesssim(\mathcal{I}_{0})^{2}+\mathcal{I}_{0}+1.

Hence, following the discussion in Section
4.4

we obtain our main existence result. Namely, one has that

Theorem 4

(
Existence result
)
.

Given
\mathcal{I}
, there exists a sufficiently large

a_{0}=a_{0}(\mathcal{I})
, such that for
0\leq a_{0}\leq a
and initial
data

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\equiv\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{15}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\mathchar 1278\relax^{j}(|u_{\infty}|\mathcal{D})^{i}(\sigma,\varphi_{0})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}\leq\mathcal{I},

along the outgoing initial null hypersurface
u=u_{\infty}
and
Minkowskian initial data along the ingoing initial null
hypersurface
v=0
, then the Einstein-scalar field system has a
solution in the causal diamond

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}=\big\{(u,v)|u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-a/4,\ \ 0\leq v\leq 1\big\}.

8
Trapped surface formation

We are now in a position to provide the main argument for the
formation of trapped surfaces in the existence domain
\mathscr{D}
.

We fix
(x^{2},x^{3})\in\mathcal{S}
and carry out a pointwise
analysis. From the estimate of
{\underline{\omega}}
,

\displaystyle||{\underline{\omega}}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}

and
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}Q={\underline{\omega}}Q
, we have that

\displaystyle|Q|\lesssim 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}||\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}Q||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

so that

\displaystyle|Q|-1\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{O}}{|u|}.

This means
Q
and
Q^{-1}
are both close to
1
.

For
P^{\mathcal{A}}
, we use the equation

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax P^{\mathcal{A}}=\rho P^{\mathcal{A}}+\sigma\bar{P}^{\mathcal{A}}

and obtain that

\displaystyle|P^{\mathcal{A}}(u,v)|\lesssim

\displaystyle|P^{\mathcal{A}}(u,0)|+\int_{0}^{v}|\mathchar 1278\relax P^{\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|P^{\mathcal{A}}(u,0)|+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{O}.

This means that in the course of the evolution
P^{\mathcal{A}}

remains close to its initial value.

For
C^{\mathcal{A}}
we use the equation

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax C^{\mathcal{A}}=(\bar{\tau}+\pi)P^{\mathcal{A}}+(\tau+\bar{\pi})\bar{P}^{\mathcal{A}},

so that

\displaystyle|C^{\mathcal{A}}|\lesssim\int_{0}^{v}|\mathchar 1278\relax C^{\mathcal{A}}|\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}.

Now, defining

\displaystyle F\equiv|u|^{2}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2}).

and using
{\bm{n}}=\partial_{u}+C^{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{\mathcal{A}}
, we
have

\displaystyle\partial_{u}F=\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}F-C^{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{\mathcal{A}}F\equiv I_{1}+I_{2}.

We now proceed to estimate the terms
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
.

Estimate for
I_{1}
.
For
\sigma
,
\varphi_{0}
and
Q
we
make use of their
\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}
equations

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\sigma

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax\tau-\mu\sigma-\bar{\lambda}\rho-\tau^{2}+{\underline{\omega}}\sigma-3\varphi_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathchar 1264\relax\bar{\varphi}_{1}+({\underline{\omega}}-\mu)\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}-\bar{\tau}\varphi_{1}-\tau\bar{\varphi}_{1},

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}Q

\displaystyle={\underline{\omega}}Q,

so that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}F

\displaystyle=|u|^{2}Q^{-1}[-\frac{2\sigma\bar{\sigma}+6\varphi_{0}^{2}}{|u|}+{\underline{\omega}}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})+\sigma\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\bar{\sigma}+\bar{\sigma}\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\sigma+6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{0}]

\displaystyle=|u|^{2}Q^{-1}(-2{\tilde{\mu}}\sigma\bar{\sigma}-6{\tilde{\mu}}\varphi_{0}^{2}+6\rho\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}-3\sigma\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}-\lambda\rho\sigma-3\bar{\sigma}\varphi_{1}^{2}-\bar{\lambda}\rho\bar{\sigma}-6\tau\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}

\displaystyle+\bar{\sigma}\tau^{2}-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\bar{\tau}-\sigma\bar{\tau}^{2}+9{\underline{\omega}}\varphi_{0}^{2}+3{\underline{\omega}}\sigma\bar{\sigma}+\bar{\sigma}\mathchar 1264\relax\tau+6\varphi_{0}\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}+\sigma\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\bar{\tau})

\displaystyle=|u|^{2}Q^{-1}M,

where we have made use of
{\tilde{\mu}}=\mu+1/|u|
. Next, we make use of the
results in Section
5
and obtain

\displaystyle|M|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{4}}.

It follows from the latter that

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}.

Estimate for
I_{2}
.
In this case we make use of the
constraint
\delta Q=(\tau-\bar{\pi})Q
and that on
\mathcal{S}_{u,v}

one has

\displaystyle|\partial_{\mathcal{A}}f|\leq||\mathcal{D}f||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})},

so that

\displaystyle|I_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq|u|^{2}|C^{\mathcal{A}}|\cdot|\partial_{\mathcal{A}}(Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2}))|

\displaystyle\leq|u|^{2}|C^{\mathcal{A}}|\cdot(||\mathcal{D}Q^{-1}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+|Q^{-1}|\cdot||\mathcal{D}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\leq|u|^{2}|C^{\mathcal{A}}|\cdot|Q^{-1}|\cdot(||\tau,\pi||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}||(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}+||\mathcal{D}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})})

\displaystyle\lesssim|u|^{2}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{O}(\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}})\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}\mathcal{O}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}.

Combining the results above we obtain

\displaystyle|\partial_{u}F|\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\ll\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|^{2}},

so that

\displaystyle-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|^{2}}\leq\partial_{u}F\leq\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|^{2}}.

The latter, in turn, leads to

\displaystyle|u|^{2}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|_{u,v}-|u_{\infty}|^{2}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|_{u=u_{\infty},v}\geq-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|}+\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u_{\infty}|}.

Hence, for sufficiently large
a
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u|^{2}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|\right)(u,v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u_{\infty}|^{2}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|\right)(u_{\infty},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|}

\displaystyle\geq a-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{|u|}\geq a-\frac{4a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{a}\geq\frac{a}{2}.

Consequently, when
u=-\frac{a}{4}
we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{a^{2}}{16}\int_{0}^{1}\left(Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|\right)(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\geq\frac{a}{2}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\int_{0}^{1}\left(Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|\right)(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\geq\frac{8}{a}.

Behaviour of the expansion.
In this step we make use of the
structure equation for
\rho
—namely,

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\rho=\rho^{2}+\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2}.

Recall that the outgoing expansion
\theta_{{\bm{l}}}
is defined by

\displaystyle\theta_{{\bm{l}}}\equiv\sigma^{ab}\nabla_{a}\l_{b}=-\rho-\bar{\rho}=-2\rho.

Its initial value is Minkowskian, namely we have that

\displaystyle\rho(u,0,x^{2},x^{3})=-\frac{1}{|u|}.

We now show that the outgoing expansion on
\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}

is positive. From
{\bm{l}}=Q\partial_{v}=\partial_{v}
, observing that
Q=1

on
\mathcal{N}_{u_{\infty}}
, we have

\displaystyle\rho(u_{\infty},v,x^{2},x^{3})

\displaystyle=\rho(u_{\infty},0,x^{2},x^{3})+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\partial\rho}{\partial v}\mathrm{d}v=-\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\int_{0}^{v}\mathchar 1278\relax\rho\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\int_{0}^{v}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim-\frac{1}{|u_{\infty}|}+\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|^{2}}<0,

where we have made use of the estimates for
\sigma
,
\varphi_{0}

and
a<|u_{\infty}|
. It follows that the outgoing expansion on the
initial outgoing lightcone is positive.

Turning now the attention to
\mathcal{N}_{-a/4}
we have that

\displaystyle\rho(-\frac{a}{4},1,x^{2},x^{3})=

\displaystyle\rho(-\frac{a}{4},0,x^{2},x^{3})+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\partial\rho}{\partial v}\mathrm{d}v=-\frac{4}{a}+\int_{0}^{1}Q^{-1}\mathchar 1278\relax\rho\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{4}{a}+\int_{0}^{1}Q^{-1}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})(-\frac{a}{4},v^{\prime},x^{2},x^{3})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{4}{a}+\frac{8}{a}=\frac{4}{a}>0.

It follows then that the outgoing expansion on
\mathcal{N}_{-a/4}
is
negative.

Recall now that the ingoing expansion
\theta_{{\bm{n}}}
is defined by

\displaystyle\theta_{{\bm{n}}}\equiv\sigma^{ab}\nabla_{a}n_{b}=\mu+\bar{\mu}=2\mu.

From the estimate

\displaystyle||{\tilde{\mu}}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}=||\mu+\frac{1}{|u|}||_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{S}_{u,v})}\leq\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},

we have

\displaystyle\mu(-\frac{a}{4},v,x^{2},x^{3})<0,\quad\mu(u_{\infty},v,x^{2},x^{3})<0

for any
(x^{2},x^{3})
. This means that the ingoing expansion is always
negative.

In conclusion, for any point on
\mathcal{S}_{-\frac{a}{4},1}
,
both
\theta_{{\bm{l}}}
and
\theta_{{\bm{n}}}
are negative. Consequently

\mathcal{S}_{-\frac{a}{4},1}
is a trapped surface. Summarising, we
have proven the following:

Theorem 5

(
Trapped surface formation
)
.

Given
\mathcal{I}
, there exists a sufficiently large

a=a(\mathcal{I})
such that if
0\leq a_{0}\leq a
, and the initial
data satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{0}\equiv\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{15}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||\mathchar 1278\relax^{j}(|u_{\infty}|\mathcal{D})^{i}(\sigma,\varphi_{0})||_{L^{2}(\mathcal{S}_{u_{\infty},v})}\leq\mathcal{I}

along the outgoing initial null hypersurface
u=u_{\infty}
and is
Minkowskian initial data along ingoing initial null
hypersurface
v=0
, with

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\left(|u_{\infty}|^{2}(\sigma\bar{\sigma}+3\varphi_{0}^{2})|\right)(u_{\infty},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\geq a,

uniformly for any point on the outgoing initial null hypersurface

u=u_{\infty}
, then
we have that
\mathcal{S}_{-a/4,1}
is a trapped surface.

Appendix A
The Einstein field equations in the NP formalism

In this appendix we provide an overview of the main geometric objects
and equations used in the analysis in the main text.

A.1
The NP field equations

Given a NP frame
\{l^{a},\ n^{a},\ m^{a},\ \bar{m}^{a}\}
, we define the
complex spin connection coefficients in the usual manner as

\displaystyle\kappa\equiv-m^{a}l^{b}\nabla_{b}l_{a},\ \ \rho\equiv-m^{a}\bar{m}^{b}\nabla_{b}l_{a},\ \ \sigma\equiv-m^{a}m^{b}\nabla_{b}l_{a},\ \ \tau\equiv-m^{a}n^{b}\nabla_{b}l_{a},

\displaystyle\nu\equiv\bar{m}^{a}n^{b}\nabla_{b}n_{a},\ \ \mu\equiv\bar{m}^{a}m^{b}\nabla_{b}n_{a},\ \ \lambda\equiv\bar{m}^{a}\bar{m}^{b}\nabla_{b}n_{a},\ \ \pi=\bar{m}^{a}l^{b}\nabla_{b}n_{a},

\displaystyle\alpha\equiv\frac{1}{2}(l^{a}\bar{m}^{b}\nabla_{b}n_{a}-m^{a}\bar{m}^{b}\nabla_{b}\bar{m}_{a}),\ \ \beta\equiv\frac{1}{2}(\bar{m}^{a}m^{b}\nabla_{b}m_{a}-n^{a}m^{b}\nabla_{b}l_{a}),

\displaystyle\epsilon\equiv\frac{1}{2}(\bar{m}^{a}l^{b}\nabla_{b}m_{a}-n^{a}l^{b}\nabla_{b}l_{a}),\ \ \gamma\equiv\frac{1}{2}(l^{a}n^{b}\nabla_{b}n_{a}-m^{a}n^{b}\nabla_{b}\bar{m}_{a}).

We also write

\displaystyle D\equiv l^{a}\nabla_{a},\qquad\Delta\equiv n^{a}\nabla_{a},\qquad\delta\equiv m^{a}\nabla_{a},\qquad\bar{\delta}=\bar{m}^{a}\nabla_{a}.

Then the commutators of the above directional covariant derivatives
satisfy

\displaystyle(\Delta D-D\Delta)\psi=\big((\gamma+\bar{\gamma})D+(\epsilon+\bar{\epsilon})\Delta-(\bar{\tau}+\pi)\delta-(\tau+\bar{\pi})\bar{\delta}\big)\psi,

(53a)

\displaystyle(\delta D-D\delta)\psi=\big((\bar{\alpha}+\beta-\bar{\pi})D+\kappa\Delta-(\bar{\rho}+\epsilon-\bar{\epsilon})\delta-\sigma\bar{\delta}\big)\psi,

(53b)

\displaystyle(\delta\Delta-\Delta\delta)\psi=\big(-\bar{\nu}D+(\tau-\bar{\alpha}-\beta)\Delta+(\mu-\gamma+\bar{\gamma})\delta+\bar{\lambda}\bar{\delta}\big)\psi,

(53c)

\displaystyle(\bar{\delta}\delta-\delta\bar{\delta})\psi=\big((\bar{\mu}-\mu)D+(\bar{\rho}-\rho)\Delta+(\alpha-\bar{\beta})\delta-(\bar{\alpha}-\beta)\bar{\delta}\big)\psi.,

(53d)

where
\psi
is any scalar field.

We use the same notation in
[
19
]
to denote the components of
Weyl spinor
\Psi_{ABCD}
and tracefree Ricci spinor

\Phi_{AA^{\prime}BB^{\prime}}
—namely {
\Psi_{0}
,
\Psi_{1}
,

\Psi_{2}
,
\Psi_{3}
,
\Psi_{4}
} and {
\Phi_{00}
,
\Phi_{01}
,

\Phi_{02}
,
\Phi_{11}
,
\Phi_{12}
,
\Phi_{22}
,
\Lambda
}. These
coefficients are defined as follows:

\displaystyle\Psi_{0}\equiv C_{abcd}l^{a}m^{b}l^{c}m^{d},\ \ \Psi_{1}\equiv C_{abcd}l^{a}n^{b}l^{c}m^{d},\ \ \Psi_{2}\equiv\frac{1}{2}C_{abcd}l^{a}n^{b}(l^{c}n^{d}-m^{c}\bar{m}^{d})

\displaystyle\Psi_{3}\equiv C_{abcd}n^{a}l^{b}n^{c}\bar{m}^{d},\ \ \Psi_{4}\equiv C_{abcd}n^{a}\bar{m}^{b}n^{c}\bar{m}^{d},

\displaystyle\Phi_{00}\equiv\frac{1}{2}R_{\{ab\}}l^{a}l^{b},\ \ \Phi_{01}\equiv\frac{1}{2}R_{\{ab\}}l^{a}m^{b},\ \ \Phi_{02}\equiv\frac{1}{2}R_{\{ab\}}m^{a}m^{b},

\displaystyle\Phi_{11}\equiv\frac{1}{4}R_{\{ab\}}(l^{a}n^{b}+m^{a}\bar{m}^{b}),\ \ \Phi_{12}\equiv\frac{1}{2}R_{\{ab\}}n^{a}m^{b},

\displaystyle\Phi_{22}\equiv\frac{1}{2}R_{\{ab\}}n^{a}n^{b},\ \ \Lambda\equiv\frac{R}{24},

where
R_{\{ab\}}=R_{ab}-\frac{1}{4}Rg_{ab}
. The NP structure
equations and the Bianchi identities can be found in
e.g.
[
15
]
.

A.2
The massless scalar equations

Given a massless scalar field
\varphi
let

\displaystyle\varphi_{0}=D\varphi,\qquad\varphi_{2}=\Delta\varphi,\qquad\varphi_{1}=\delta\varphi,\qquad\bar{\varphi}_{1}=\bar{\delta}\varphi.

Then the equation
\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}\varphi=0
can be expressed in
terms of the NP frame. One finds that

\displaystyle D\varphi_{2}+\Delta\varphi_{0}-\delta\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\bar{\delta}\varphi_{1}+\bar{\varphi}_{1}(\bar{\alpha}-\beta)+\varphi_{1}(\alpha-\bar{\beta})

\displaystyle-\varphi_{0}(\gamma+\bar{\gamma})+\varphi_{2}(\epsilon+\bar{\epsilon})+\varphi_{0}(\mu+\bar{\mu})-\bar{\varphi}_{1}\bar{\pi}-\varphi_{1}\pi

\displaystyle-\varphi_{2}(\rho+\bar{\rho})+\varphi_{1}\bar{\tau}+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\tau=0.

Applying the commutator
relations (
53a
)-(
53d
), one has that

\displaystyle\Delta\varphi_{0}-\bar{\delta}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle=(\gamma+\bar{\gamma}-\bar{\mu})\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}+(\bar{\beta}-\alpha-\bar{\tau})\varphi_{1}-\tau\bar{\varphi}_{1},

(54a)

\displaystyle\Delta\varphi_{1}-\delta\varphi_{2}

\displaystyle=\bar{\nu}\varphi_{0}+(\bar{\alpha}+\beta-\tau)\varphi_{2}+(\gamma-\bar{\gamma}-\mu)\varphi_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

(54b)

\displaystyle D\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\bar{\delta}\varphi_{0}

\displaystyle=(\pi-\alpha-\bar{\beta})\varphi_{0}-\bar{\kappa}\varphi_{2}+\bar{\sigma}\varphi_{1}+(\bar{\epsilon}-\epsilon+\rho)\bar{\varphi}_{1},

(54c)

\displaystyle D\varphi_{2}-\bar{\delta}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle=-\bar{\mu}\varphi_{0}+(\rho-\epsilon-\bar{\epsilon})\varphi_{2}+(\bar{\beta}-\alpha+\pi)\varphi_{1}+\bar{\pi}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

(54d)

\displaystyle\delta\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\bar{\delta}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle=(\mu-\bar{\mu})\varphi_{0}+(\rho-\bar{\rho})\varphi_{2}+(\bar{\beta}-\alpha)\varphi_{1}+(\bar{\alpha}-\beta)\bar{\varphi}_{1}.

(54e)

Now, from the Einstein-scalar field equation

\displaystyle R_{ab}=6\nabla_{a}\varphi\nabla_{b}\varphi

one obtains the relations

\displaystyle\Phi_{00}=3\varphi_{0}^{2},\quad\Phi_{01}=3\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1},\quad\Phi_{02}=3\varphi_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle\Phi_{11}=\frac{3}{2}(\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}),\quad\Phi_{12}=3\varphi_{2}\varphi_{1},\quad\Phi_{22}=3\varphi_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\Lambda=\frac{1}{2}(-\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}).

Now, making use of the definitions of the renormalised components of the
Weyl tensor —namely

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{1}\equiv

\displaystyle\Psi_{1}-\Phi_{01}=\Psi_{1}-3\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{2}\equiv

\displaystyle\Psi_{2}+2\Lambda=\Psi_{2}-\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{3}\equiv

\displaystyle\Psi_{3}-\Phi_{21}=\Psi_{3}-3\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1},

and substituting into the structure equations and Bianchi identities
one can obtain the NP form of the Einstein-Massless scalar system.
The structure equations take the form

\displaystyle\Delta\epsilon-D\gamma=

\displaystyle-\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-3\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}+\epsilon(2\gamma+\bar{\gamma})+\gamma\bar{\epsilon}+\kappa\nu-\beta\pi-\alpha\bar{\pi}-\alpha\tau-\pi\tau-\beta\bar{\tau},

(55a)

\displaystyle\Delta\kappa-D\tau=

\displaystyle-\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}+3\gamma\kappa+\bar{\gamma}\kappa-\bar{\pi}\rho-\pi\sigma-\epsilon\tau+\bar{\epsilon}\tau-\rho\tau-\sigma\bar{\tau},

(55b)

\displaystyle\Delta\pi-D\nu=

\displaystyle-\tilde{\Psi}_{3}-6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+3\epsilon\nu+\bar{\epsilon}\nu-\gamma\pi+\bar{\gamma}\pi-\mu\pi-\lambda\bar{\pi}-\lambda\tau-\mu\bar{\tau},

(55c)

\displaystyle\delta\gamma-\Delta\beta=

\displaystyle 3\varphi_{2}\varphi_{1}-\bar{\alpha}\gamma-2\beta\gamma+\beta\bar{\gamma}+\alpha\bar{\lambda}+\beta\mu-\epsilon\bar{\nu}-\nu\sigma+\gamma\tau+\mu\tau,

(55d)

\displaystyle\delta\epsilon-D\beta=

\displaystyle-\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-3\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}+\bar{\alpha}\epsilon+\beta\bar{\epsilon}+\gamma\kappa+\kappa\mu-\epsilon\bar{\pi}-\beta\bar{\rho}-\alpha\sigma-\pi\sigma,

(55e)

\displaystyle\delta\kappa-D\sigma=

\displaystyle-\Psi_{0}+\bar{\alpha}\kappa+3\beta\kappa-\kappa\bar{\pi}-3\epsilon\sigma+\bar{\epsilon}\sigma-\rho\sigma-\bar{\rho}\sigma+\kappa\tau,

(55f)

\displaystyle\delta\nu-\Delta\mu=

\displaystyle 3\varphi_{2}^{2}+\lambda\bar{\lambda}+\gamma\mu+\bar{\gamma}\mu+\mu^{2}-\bar{\alpha}\nu-3\beta\nu-\bar{\nu}\pi+\nu\tau,

(55g)

\displaystyle\delta\pi-D\mu=

\displaystyle-\tilde{\Psi}_{2}+\epsilon\mu+\bar{\epsilon}\mu+\kappa\nu+\bar{\alpha}\pi-\beta\pi-\pi\bar{\pi}-\mu\bar{\rho}-\lambda\sigma,

(55h)

\displaystyle\delta\tau-\Delta\sigma=

\displaystyle 3\varphi_{1}^{2}-\kappa\bar{\nu}+\bar{\lambda}\rho-3\gamma\sigma+\bar{\gamma}\sigma+\mu\sigma-\bar{\alpha}\tau+\beta\tau+\tau^{2},

(55i)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\beta-\delta\alpha=

\displaystyle-3\bar{\varphi}_{1}\varphi_{1}+\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-\alpha\bar{\alpha}+2\alpha\beta-\beta\bar{\beta}-\epsilon\mu+\epsilon\bar{\mu}-\gamma\rho-\mu\rho+\gamma\bar{\rho}+\lambda\sigma,

(55j)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\gamma-\Delta\alpha=

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{3}+3\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-\bar{\beta}\gamma-\alpha\bar{\gamma}+\beta\lambda+\alpha\bar{\mu}-\epsilon\nu-\nu\rho+\lambda\tau+\gamma\bar{\tau},

(55k)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\epsilon-D\alpha=

\displaystyle-3\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}+2\alpha\epsilon+\bar{\beta}\epsilon-\alpha\bar{\epsilon}+\gamma\bar{\kappa}+\kappa\lambda-\epsilon\pi-\alpha\rho-\pi\rho-\beta\bar{\sigma},

(55l)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\kappa-D\rho=

\displaystyle-3\varphi_{0}^{2}+3\alpha\kappa+\bar{\beta}\kappa-\kappa\pi-\epsilon\rho-\bar{\epsilon}\rho-\rho^{2}-\sigma\bar{\sigma}+\bar{\kappa}\tau,

(55m)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\mu-\delta\lambda=

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{3}-\bar{\alpha}\lambda+3\beta\lambda-\alpha\mu-\bar{\beta}\mu-\mu\pi+\bar{\mu}\pi-\nu\rho+\nu\bar{\rho},

(55n)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\nu-\Delta\lambda=

\displaystyle\Psi_{4}+3\gamma\lambda-\bar{\gamma}\lambda+\lambda\mu+\lambda\bar{\mu}-3\alpha\nu-\bar{\beta}\nu-\nu\pi+\nu\bar{\tau},

(55o)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\pi-D\lambda=

\displaystyle-3\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}+3\epsilon\lambda-\bar{\epsilon}\lambda+\bar{\kappa}\nu-\alpha\pi+\bar{\beta}\pi-\pi^{2}-\lambda\rho-\mu\bar{\sigma},

(55p)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\sigma-\delta\rho=

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-\kappa\mu+\kappa\bar{\mu}-\bar{\alpha}\rho-\beta\rho+3\alpha\sigma-\bar{\beta}\sigma-\rho\tau-\bar{\rho}\tau,

(55q)

\displaystyle\bar{\delta}\tau-\Delta\rho=

\displaystyle\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-\kappa\nu-\gamma\rho-\bar{\gamma}\rho+\bar{\mu}\rho+\lambda\sigma+\alpha\tau-\bar{\beta}\tau+\tau\bar{\tau}.

(55r)

The Bianchi identities written in terms of the renormalised components
of the Weyl tensor take the following decoupled form:

\displaystyle\Delta\Psi_{0}-\delta\tilde{\Psi}_{1}=

\displaystyle 6\varphi_{0}\delta\varphi_{1}+(4\gamma-\mu)\Psi_{0}-(2\beta+4\tau)\tilde{\Psi}_{1}+3\sigma\tilde{\Psi}_{2}

\displaystyle+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\bar{\alpha}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}\sigma-3\varphi_{0}^{2}\bar{\lambda}-3\varphi_{1}^{2}\bar{\rho}-6\bar{\varphi}_{1}\varphi_{1}\sigma-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}(\beta+2\tau),

(56a)

\displaystyle D\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-\bar{\delta}\Psi_{0}=

\displaystyle-6\varphi_{0}\delta\varphi_{0}+(\pi-4\alpha)\Psi_{0}+(2\epsilon+4\rho)\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-3\kappa\tilde{\Psi}_{2}

\displaystyle+3\varphi_{0}^{2}(2\bar{\alpha}+2\beta-\bar{\pi})+6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\kappa+3\varphi_{1}^{2}\bar{\kappa}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}(2\rho-\bar{\rho})+6\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\sigma,

(56b)

\displaystyle\Delta\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-\delta\tilde{\Psi}_{2}=

\displaystyle-6\varphi_{1}\bar{\delta}\varphi_{1}+\nu\Psi_{0}+(2\gamma-2\mu)\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-3\tau\tilde{\Psi}_{2}+3\sigma\tilde{\Psi}_{3}

\displaystyle+6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\sigma+6\varphi_{1}(\varphi_{0}\bar{\mu}-\varphi_{0}\mu+\bar{\varphi}_{1}\tau)+3\varphi_{1}^{2}(2\alpha-2\bar{\beta}+\bar{\tau})

\displaystyle-3\varphi_{0}^{2}\bar{\nu}-6\varphi_{2}\varphi_{1}\rho,

(56c)

\displaystyle D\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-\bar{\delta}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}=

\displaystyle 6\varphi_{1}\bar{\delta}\varphi_{0}-\lambda\Psi_{0}+(2\pi-2\alpha)\tilde{\Psi}_{1}+3\rho\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-2\kappa\tilde{\Psi}_{3}

\displaystyle 3\varphi_{0}^{2}\bar{\mu}+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\pi+3\varphi_{1}^{2}\bar{\sigma}-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}(\alpha+\bar{\beta})-6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\kappa,

(56d)

\displaystyle\Delta\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-\delta\tilde{\Psi}_{3}=

\displaystyle 6\bar{\varphi}_{1}\delta\varphi_{2}+2\nu\tilde{\Psi}_{1}-3\mu\tilde{\Psi}_{2}+(2\beta-2\tau)\tilde{\Psi}_{3}+\sigma\Psi_{4}

\displaystyle+6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}(\bar{\alpha}+\beta-\tau)+6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\nu-3\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}\bar{\lambda}-3\varphi_{2}^{2}\bar{\rho},

(56e)

\displaystyle D\tilde{\Psi}_{3}-\bar{\delta}\tilde{\Psi}_{2}=

\displaystyle 6\bar{\varphi}_{1}\delta\bar{\varphi}_{1}-2\lambda\tilde{\Psi}_{1}+3\pi\tilde{\Psi}_{2}+(2\rho-2\epsilon)\tilde{\Psi}_{3}-\kappa\Psi_{4}

\displaystyle+3\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}(2\bar{\alpha}-2\beta-\bar{\pi})+3\varphi_{2}^{2}\bar{\kappa}+6\varphi_{0}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\mu+6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}(\rho-\bar{\rho})

\displaystyle-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{1}\lambda+6\bar{\varphi}_{1}\varphi_{1}\pi,

(56f)

\displaystyle\Delta\tilde{\Psi}_{3}-\delta\Psi_{4}=

\displaystyle-6\varphi_{2}\bar{\delta}\varphi_{2}+3\nu\tilde{\Psi}_{2}-(2\gamma+4\mu)\tilde{\Psi}_{3}+(4\beta-\tau)\Psi_{4}

\displaystyle+6\varphi_{2}\varphi_{1}\lambda+6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}(\bar{\mu}-2\mu)+3\varphi_{2}^{2}(\bar{\tau}-2\alpha-2\bar{\beta})-6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\nu-3\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}\bar{\nu},

(56g)

\displaystyle D\Psi_{4}-\bar{\delta}\tilde{\Psi}_{3}=

\displaystyle 6\varphi_{2}\bar{\delta}\bar{\varphi}_{1}-3\lambda\tilde{\Psi}_{2}+(2\alpha+4\pi)\tilde{\Psi}_{3}+(\rho-4\epsilon)\Psi_{4}

\displaystyle 6\varphi_{2}\bar{\varphi}_{1}(\alpha-\bar{\beta}+2\pi)+6\varphi_{1}\bar{\varphi}_{1}\lambda+3\bar{\varphi}_{1}^{2}\mu+3\varphi_{2}^{2}\bar{\sigma}-6\varphi_{0}\varphi_{2}\lambda.

(56h)

Appendix B
NP T-weight equations

Using the T-weighted operators the structure equations can be
rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\rho-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\kappa

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}+\sigma\bar{\sigma}+\omega\rho-\bar{\kappa}\tau-(2\bar{\vartheta}-\pi)\kappa+\Phi_{00},

(57a)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\sigma-\mathchar 1264\relax\kappa

\displaystyle=(\rho+\bar{\rho})\sigma+\omega\sigma-(\tau-\bar{\pi}+2\vartheta)\kappa+\Psi_{0},

(57b)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\mu-\mathchar 1264\relax\pi

\displaystyle=\bar{\rho}\mu+\sigma\lambda+\pi\bar{\pi}-\omega\mu-\nu\kappa+\Psi_{2}+2\Lambda,

(57c)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\lambda-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\pi

\displaystyle=\rho\lambda+\bar{\sigma}\mu+\pi^{2}-\nu\bar{\kappa}-\omega\lambda+\Phi_{20},

(57d)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\rho-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\tau

\displaystyle=-\rho\bar{\mu}-\sigma\lambda-\bar{\tau}\tau+{\underline{\omega}}\rho-\nu\kappa-\Psi_{2}-2\Lambda,

(57e)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\sigma-\mathchar 1264\relax\tau

\displaystyle=-\mu\sigma-\bar{\lambda}\rho-\tau^{2}+{\underline{\omega}}\sigma+\kappa\bar{\nu}-\Phi_{02},

(57f)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\mu-\mathchar 1264\relax\nu

\displaystyle=-\mu^{2}-\lambda\bar{\lambda}-{\underline{\omega}}\mu+\bar{\nu}\pi+(2\vartheta-\tau)\nu-\Phi_{22},

(57g)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\lambda-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\nu

\displaystyle=-(\mu+\bar{\mu})\lambda-{\underline{\omega}}\lambda+(2\bar{\vartheta}+\pi-\bar{\tau})\nu-\Psi_{4},

(57h)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\tau-\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\kappa

\displaystyle=(\tau+\bar{\pi})\rho+(\bar{\tau}+\pi)\sigma-2{\underline{\omega}}\kappa+\Psi_{1}+\Phi_{01},

(57i)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\pi-\mathchar 1278\relax\nu

\displaystyle=-(\pi+\bar{\tau})\mu-(\bar{\pi}+\tau)\lambda+2\omega\nu-\Psi_{3}-\Phi_{21},

(57j)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax{\underline{\omega}}-\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\omega

\displaystyle=(\tau+\bar{\pi})\bar{\vartheta}+(\bar{\tau}+\pi)\vartheta-2{\underline{\omega}}\omega+\tau\pi+\bar{\tau}\bar{\pi}

\displaystyle\quad-\nu\kappa-\bar{\nu}\bar{\kappa}+\Psi_{2}+\bar{\Psi}_{2}+2\Phi_{11}-2\Lambda,

(57k)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\vartheta-\mathchar 1264\relax\omega

\displaystyle=(\bar{\vartheta}+\pi)\sigma+(\vartheta+\bar{\pi})\bar{\rho}+(\bar{\pi}-\vartheta)\omega

\displaystyle\quad-(\mu+{\underline{\omega}})\kappa-\bar{\kappa}\bar{\lambda}+\Psi_{1}+\Phi_{01},

(57l)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\vartheta-\mathchar 1264\relax{\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle=-(\bar{\tau}+\bar{\vartheta})\bar{\lambda}-(\tau+\vartheta)\mu-(\tau-\vartheta){\underline{\omega}}

\displaystyle\quad+(\bar{\rho}+\omega)\bar{\nu}+\sigma\nu-\bar{\Psi}_{3}-\Phi_{12},

(57m)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1264\relax\rho-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\sigma

\displaystyle=\vartheta\rho-\bar{\vartheta}\sigma+(\rho-\bar{\rho})\tau+(\mu-\bar{\mu})\kappa-\Psi_{1}+\Phi_{01},

(57n)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1264\relax\lambda-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\mu

\displaystyle=\bar{\vartheta}\mu-\vartheta\lambda+(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nu+(\mu-\bar{\mu})\pi-\Psi_{3}+\Phi_{21},

(57o)

while for the Bianchi identities one has

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\Psi_{1}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{0}-\mathchar 1278\relax\Phi_{01}+\mathchar 1264\relax\Phi_{00}=

\displaystyle(\pi-2\bar{\vartheta})\Psi_{0}+(\omega+4\rho)\Psi_{1}-(\omega+2\bar{\rho})\Phi_{01}+(2\vartheta-\bar{\pi})\Phi_{00}

\displaystyle-3\kappa\Psi_{2}-2\sigma\Phi_{10}+2\kappa\Phi_{11}+\bar{\kappa}\Phi_{02},

(58a)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{0}-\mathchar 1264\relax\Psi_{1}+\mathchar 1278\relax\Phi_{02}-\mathchar 1264\relax\Phi_{01}=

\displaystyle(2{\underline{\omega}}-\mu)\Psi_{0}-(4\tau+\vartheta)\Psi_{1}+\bar{\rho}\Phi_{02}+(2\pi-\vartheta)\Phi_{01}

\displaystyle+3\sigma\Psi_{2}+2\sigma\Phi_{11}-2\kappa\Phi_{12}-\bar{\lambda}\Phi_{00},

(58b)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\Psi_{2}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Psi_{1}+\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Phi_{00}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Phi_{01}+2\mathchar 1278\relax\Lambda=

\displaystyle 3\rho\Psi_{2}+(2\pi-\bar{\vartheta})\Psi_{1}+(2{\underline{\omega}}-\bar{\mu})\Phi_{00}-(2\bar{\tau}+\bar{\vartheta})\Phi_{01}

\displaystyle+({\underline{\omega}}-2\bar{\mu}-\mu)\Phi_{01}+\bar{\nu}\Phi_{00}-\bar{\lambda}\Phi_{10}+\sigma\Phi_{21}-\kappa\Phi_{22},

(58j)

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax\Phi_{22}-\mathchar 1264\relax\Phi_{21}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\Phi_{12}+\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Phi_{11}+3\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\Lambda=

\displaystyle-2(\mu+\bar{\mu})\Phi_{11}+\nu\Phi_{01}+\bar{\nu}\Phi_{10}-\bar{\lambda}\Phi_{20}-\lambda\Phi_{02}.

(58k)

The commutator relations for any quantity

B.1

system in the T-weight formalism.

We various components of the auxiliary field
\varphi_{a}
encoding the

\varphi

Recalling that

\displaystyle\mathchar 1278\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{0}-\mathchar 1264\relax^{\prime}\varphi_{1}

\displaystyle=({\underline{\omega}}-\bar{\mu})\varphi_{0}+\rho\varphi_{2}-\bar{\tau}\varphi_{1}-\tau\bar{\varphi}_{1},

Appendix C
Scaling argument

Consider a conformal transformation from a
spacetime
(\mathcal{M},{\bm{g}})
to another
spacetime
(\mathcal{M}^{\prime},{\bm{g}}^{\prime})
of the form

\displaystyle{\bm{g}}^{\prime}=\delta^{2}{\bm{g}},

(66)

where
\delta
is a constant —i.e. a
homotety
. This conformal
transformation leaves the scalar field invariant. Now, define a
coordinate system
(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},x^{\prime\mathcal{A}})
on
\mathcal{M}^{\prime}
via the
relations

\displaystyle x^{\prime\mu}(p^{\prime})\equiv\delta x^{\mu}(p)\Rightarrow u^{\prime}\equiv\delta u,\quad v^{\prime}\equiv\delta v,\quad x^{\prime\mathcal{A}}\equiv\delta x^{\mathcal{A}},

where
p^{\prime}
is the image of
p
under the conformal
transformation. Under this transformation the domain

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}=\bigg\{p\in\mathcal{M}\;|\;u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-\frac{a}{4},\;\;0\leq v\leq 1\bigg\}

is mapped to

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}^{\prime}=\bigg\{p^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}^{\prime}\;|\;\delta u_{\infty}\leq u^{\prime}\leq-\frac{\delta a}{4},\;\;0\leq v^{\prime}\leq\delta\bigg\}.

As the scalar field is not derived from the metric, we can choose it
to be invariant —i.e. we set

\displaystyle\varphi(x^{\prime})=\varphi(x).

Moreover, we also have that

\displaystyle Q^{\prime}=Q,\qquad C^{\prime\mathcal{A}}=C^{\mathcal{A}},\qquad P^{\prime\mathcal{A}}=P^{\mathcal{A}}.

On the other hand, the rescaling (
66
) implies
for the NP frame that

\displaystyle{\bm{l}}^{\prime}=\delta^{-1}{\bm{l}},\quad{\bm{n}}^{\prime}=\delta^{-1}{\bm{n}},\quad{\bm{m}}^{\prime}=\delta^{-1}{\bm{m}},\quad\bar{\bm{m}}^{\prime}=\delta^{-1}\bar{{\bm{m}}^{\prime}}.

In addition, we also have that
\nabla^{\prime}_{a}=\nabla_{a}
. Then from the
definition of the NP connection coefficients we have
that
\Gamma^{\prime}=\delta^{-1}\Gamma
. For the curvature we
have
R^{\prime a}_{\phantom{{}^{\prime}a}bcd}=R^{a}_{\phantom{a}bcd}
, so
that
R^{\prime}_{abcd}=\delta^{2}R_{abcd}
which, in turn, leads
to
\Psi^{\prime}=\delta^{-2}\Psi
. Taking into account the rescaling
properties of the frame it follows that the components of the
derivative of the scalar field satisfy

\displaystyle\varphi^{\prime}_{0}=\delta^{-1}\varphi_{0},\qquad\varphi^{\prime}_{1}=\delta^{-1}\varphi_{1},\qquad\varphi^{\prime}_{2}=\delta^{-1}\varphi_{2}.

From the estimates in the main text and considering
that
\frac{a\delta}{4}\leq|u^{\prime}|\leq\delta|u_{\infty}|
, one can
readily see that

\Gamma^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle|\rho^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}|\rho(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{1}{|u|}=\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|},\quad|\sigma^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-1}|\sigma(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}=\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|},

\displaystyle|\mu^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}|\mu(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{1}{|u|}=\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|},\quad|{\tilde{\mu}}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})=\mu^{\prime}+\frac{1}{|u^{\prime}|}|=\delta^{-1}|{\tilde{\mu}}(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{\delta}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}},

\displaystyle|\lambda^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}|\lambda(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}},\quad|{\underline{\omega}}^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-1}|{\underline{\omega}}(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}=\frac{\delta^{2}a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}}\leq\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}},

\displaystyle|\tau^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}|\tau(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}},\quad|\pi^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-1}|\pi(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}.

\Psi^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle|\Psi^{\prime}_{0}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-2}|\Psi_{0}(x)|\leq\delta^{-2}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}=\frac{\delta^{-1}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|},\quad|\Psi^{\prime}_{1}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-2}|\Psi_{1}(x)|\leq\delta^{-2}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}},

\displaystyle|\Psi^{\prime}_{2}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-2}|\Psi_{2}(x)|\leq\delta^{-2}\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}=\frac{\delta a}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}},\quad|\Psi^{\prime}_{3}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-2}|\Psi_{3}(x)|\leq\delta^{-2}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}=\frac{\delta^{2}a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{4}}\leq\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{3}},

\displaystyle|\Psi^{\prime}_{4}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-2}|\Psi_{4}(x)|\leq\delta^{-2}\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{5}}=\frac{\delta^{3}a^{2}}{|u^{\prime}|^{5}}.

{\bm{\varphi}}^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle|\varphi^{\prime}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=|\varphi(x)|\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}=\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|},\quad|\varphi^{\prime}_{0}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-1}|\varphi_{0}(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}=\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|},

\displaystyle|\varphi^{\prime}_{1}(x^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\delta^{-1}|\varphi_{1}(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}},\quad|\varphi^{\prime}_{2}(x^{\prime})|=\delta^{-1}|\varphi_{2}(x)|\leq\delta^{-1}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}=\frac{\delta a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}.
